# Phase 7.3 — CMaNGOS family: data model, stage kinds, engine Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The `CmangosInstaller` and its five stage-kind modules exist, typed and unit-tested, with all four catalog entries validating, WotLK's templates byte-identical, the shared `cmangos` templates and the TBC/Vanilla/Tortoise Dockerfiles written, and the busybox/mariadb:11 primitives proven live — everything 7.4a needs to press Install for TBC.

**Architecture:** Typed catalog blocks (`CmangosData`: `ClientSpec`, `DockerfileSpec`, `ExtractPlan`, `MmapPlan`, `ConfPatchTable`, `SqlPlan`; `Source.rev`) parameterise family-neutral modules `clientdir`, `dockerfile`, `extract`, `conf`, `sqlplan`; `docker.py` gains `run_container`, `copy_from_image`, `exec_stdin`, `sql_query`; `families/cmangos.py` binds them into the twelve-stage tuple on the 7.1 spine. Client mounted read-only, per-tool extraction evidence, SQL streamed from host clones over `docker exec -i`, a completion marker gated on verify.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.11+, pydantic (catalog models), PySide6 (untouched here), Docker CLI via `subprocess` (never the SDK), pytest + mypy + ruff + black; live gates on the Hyper-V VMs `yulon-ubuntu` (Docker Engine), Fedora 44 and Arch clean boxes, `yulon-win11` (Docker Desktop/WSL2).

**Spec:** `pyplan/phase7-decisions.md` (the design and the ten gated steps; this plan is step 7.3). Also binding: `pyplan/style-guide.md`, `pyplan/README.md`. Progress is ticked in `pyplan/checklist.md` under 7.3.

**Task order (keeps `pytest`/`mypy` green at every commit):** G.1, G.2, G.3, G.4, G.5, G.6, H.1, H.2, H.3, H.4, H.5, H.6, K.1, I.1, I.2, I.3, I.4, I.5, I.6, I.7, I.8, J.1, J.2, J.3, J.4, J.5, J.6, K.2, K.3, K.4, K.5, K.6, K.7, K.8, G.7

**Commit rules:** scoped commits per task; no "Generated with Claude Code" footer, no session link, no `Co-Authored-By: Claude` trailer (project rule). Never push to `main`; work is on `yulon-phase7`.

## Global Constraints

The interface contract below is copied verbatim from the design session. Every name, signature and type in it is final; its Amendments section (A1–A18) overrides the text above it. A task may add private helpers; it may not rename, re-type or re-home anything here.

<summary>Interface contract (binding)</summary>

# Phase 7 interface contract (binding for every plan task)

Every name, signature and type below is FINAL. A task may add private helpers; it may not
rename, re-type or re-home anything here. `pylauncher/` is the package root; paths are relative
to it. Python 3.11+, fully typed, `from __future__ import annotations`, pathlib, dataclasses,
pydantic for catalog models, `logging` via `yulon.log.get_logger(__name__)`, line length 100,
black + ruff + mypy clean. Tests: pytest, no daemon for unit tests, `tests/integration/` for
Docker-backed tests (self-skipping). Never a `print`. Never `Any` except at a `json.load()`
boundary. No `sudoers.d`, no `chmod` of the docker socket, no `usermod -aG docker` without the
existing consent path.

## 7.1 — spine, AzerothCore family, Linux native

### `yulon/catalog/native.py` (the spine)

```python
STATE_FILE = ".yulon-install.json"
STATE_VERSION = 1

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Secrets:
    db_password: str
    def __repr__(self) -> str: ...          # "Secrets(db_password=***)" — never the value

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StageContext:
    server_dir: Path
    client_dir: Path | None
    state: InstallState
    cancel: threading.Event | None
    secrets: Secrets

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Stage:
    name: str
    run: Callable[[StageContext], Iterator[str]]
    recorded: bool = True
    cancel_note: str = ""

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class InstallState:
    game_id: str
    install_id: str
    family: str = ""                          # NEW; "" in files written before 7.1
    completed: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    last_error: str = ""
    updated_unix: int = 0
    version: int = STATE_VERSION
    def with_stage(self, stage: str, order: Sequence[str]) -> InstallState: ...
    def has(self, stage: str) -> bool: ...

def read_state(server_dir: Path, *, valid: Sequence[str]) -> InstallState | None: ...
def write_state(server_dir: Path, state: InstallState) -> None: ...

@dataclass
class Seams:                                 # every existing field keeps its name and type, plus:
    wait_ready: Callable[[docker.ContainerSpec, docker.ReadySpec], bool] = docker.wait_ready_for
    relabel: Callable[[Path], bool] = platform.relabel_for_containers
    selinux_enforcing: Callable[[], bool | None] = platform.selinux_enforcing
    fs_type: Callable[[Path], str | None] = platform.filesystem_type
    # ensure_docker is now CALLED WITH ask: self._seams.ensure_docker(cancel=cancel, ask=ask)

class StagedInstaller:
    """Abstract spine. Subclasses implement stages()."""
    def __init__(self, entry: CatalogEntry, *, installers_root: Path | None = None,
                 import_probe: docker.ImportProbe | None = None,
                 reset_unfinished: docker.ResetUnfinished | None = None,
                 seams: Seams | None = None) -> None: ...
    family: ClassVar[str]                     # "azerothcore" | "cmangos"; must equal entry.install.native.family
    def stages(self) -> tuple[Stage, ...]: ...            # abstract; names unique; never "preflight"/"guard"
    def stage_names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]: ...
    def server_dir(self, options: InstallOptions) -> Path: ...
    def preflight(self, options: InstallOptions, cancel: threading.Event | None = None, *,
                  ask: runner.Prompter | None = None) -> None: ...
    def run(self, options: InstallOptions | None = None, *, cancel: threading.Event | None = None,
            ask: runner.Prompter | None = None) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    # spine-provided stage bodies a family binds into Stage.run:
    def stage_clone_sources(self, ctx: StageContext, sources: Sequence[EmulatorSource]) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    def stage_generate_compose(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    def stage_build(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    def stage_start_db(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    def stage_import(self, ctx: StageContext, gate: ImportGate, service: str | None) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    def stage_up(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    def stage_ready(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]: ...
    # kept from NativeInstaller, unchanged in behaviour: _guard, _refuse_foreign_containers,
    # _preflight_lines, _pump, _check_run, _check_cancel, _held_awake, _record_error, _clone,
    # _remote_of, _replaceable_compose, _same_repo, _repo_key, _git_remote_url, _git_file_unmodified
    def resolve_secrets(self, server_dir: Path) -> Secrets: ...   # fixed → value; generated → read file or secrets.token_hex(8) with prefix

class ImportGate(Protocol):
    def probe(self) -> docker.ImportState: ...
    def reset(self) -> tuple[str, ...]: ...

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CallableGate:                          # ImportGate from two callables (the AC pair)
    probe_fn: docker.ImportProbe
    reset_fn: docker.ResetUnfinished | None
    def probe(self) -> docker.ImportState: ...
    def reset(self) -> tuple[str, ...]: ...  # raises InstallerError when reset_fn is None

# module constants kept: OPENING_NOTE, BUILD_CANCEL_NOTE, DOWNLOAD_CANCEL_NOTE, IMPORT_CANCEL_NOTE
# DELETED: NativeInstaller, STAGE_ORDER, NEVER_RECORDED, _READY_REALM_HOST, _run_stage and the
# nine _clone_core.._ready methods (their bodies move to families/azerothcore.py or become the
# stage_* methods above).
```

### `yulon/catalog/catalog.py` (7.1 subset)
```python
class EmulatorSource(Source):
    dest: str = Field(min_length=1)          # relative to the server dir; "." allowed
class Emulator(_Strict):
    sources: tuple[EmulatorSource, ...]
class PasswordPlan(_Strict):
    mode: Literal["fixed", "generated"]
    value: str | None = None                 # required when fixed
    file: str | None = None                  # required when generated, e.g. ".db_password"
    prefix: str = ""
class DbFacts(_Strict):
    image: str; client: Literal["mysql", "mariadb"]; user: str; charset: str = "utf8mb4"
class ReadyMarkers(_Strict):
    world: str; auth: str | None = None; fatal: str | None = None; timeout_s: int = 600; restart_loop: int = 4
class AzerothCoreData(_Strict):
    world_env: dict[str, str] = {}
class NativeInstall(_Strict):                 # existing fields kept (templates, soap_port, floors); world_env MOVES to azerothcore.world_env
    family: Literal["azerothcore", "cmangos"]
    images: tuple[str, ...] = Field(min_length=1)
    image_prefix: str = Field(min_length=1)
    dockerfile_dir: str | None = None
    db: DbFacts
    ready: ReadyMarkers
    azerothcore: AzerothCoreData | None = None
    # 7.3 adds: cmangos: CmangosData | None = None; validator: exactly the block `family` names is present
class Install(_Strict):
    password: PasswordPlan                   # REPLACES db_root_password / db_root_password_file (both deleted in 7.1)
    # script, script_platforms, script_variants, requires_client_dir, platforms, default_server_dir, native: unchanged in 7.1
```
catalog.json in 7.1: all four entries get `password` and source `dest`s; wow-wotlk gets the
`native` additions (`family`, `images`, `image_prefix`, `db`, `ready`, `azerothcore.world_env`)
and loses `script_platforms`; the other three keep `script` + `platforms: ["linux"]` and get NO
`native` block yet.

### `yulon/docker.py`
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReadySpec:
    world: str                                # regex, re.search over the world container's this-run log
    auth: str | None = None                   # regex over the auth log; None = do not wait on auth
    fatal: str | None = None                  # regex over the world log; a match → return False at once
    timeout: float = _READY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS
    interval: float = _POLL_INTERVAL_SECONDS
    restart_loop: int = 4                     # RestartCount growth that means a crash loop → False
def wait_ready(auth_container: str, world_container: str, spec: ReadySpec) -> bool: ...   # SIGNATURE CHANGE
AZEROTHCORE_READY_WORLD = "ready..."          # the legacy wrapper's marker
def azerothcore_ready(realm_host: str, realm_port: int, **kwargs: float) -> ReadySpec: ...  # auth=re.escape(f"{host}:{port}"), world=AZEROTHCORE_READY_WORLD
def wait_ready_for(spec: ContainerSpec, ready: ReadySpec) -> bool: ...                     # SIGNATURE CHANGE
# controller.py:265 and controller_wow_wotlk/docker_ctl.py:53 call wait_ready_for(spec, azerothcore_ready(host, port, **kwargs))
# test_docker.py's four direct wait_ready callers pass ReadySpec.
```

### `yulon/platform.py`
```python
SELINUX_NOLABEL_FS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"exfat","ntfs","ntfs3","fuseblk","msdos","vfat","cifs","smb2","nfs","nfs4","9p"})
def selinux_enforcing(*, enforce_path: Path = Path("/sys/fs/selinux/enforce"), run: RunCmd | None = None,
                      which: Callable[[str], str | None] | None = None) -> bool | None: ...   # None = could not tell / not Linux
def filesystem_type(path: Path, *, run: RunCmd | None = None) -> str | None: ...           # `stat -f -c %T <path>`
def selinux_labels_supported(fs_type: str | None) -> bool: ...                               # None → True (deny-list)
def relabel_for_containers(path: Path, *, run: RunCmd | None = None,
                           which: Callable[[str], str | None] | None = None) -> bool: ...   # chcon -Rt container_file_t, no sudo
def bind_label(*, enforcing: bool | None, fs_type: str | None) -> str: ...                   # ":z" or ""
def container_user_args(*, platform_id: Callable[[], PlatformId] = detect) -> list[str]: ...  # ["--user", "uid:gid"] on linux, [] elsewhere; git.ContainerGit._user_args calls this
def keep_awake(...)  # Linux branch: spawn ["systemd-inhibit", "--what=idle:sleep", "--who=Yu'lon", "--why=installing a server", "sleep", "infinity"] detached; terminate on exit; OSError → warning + yield
def docker_engine_commands(pm, *, steamos) -> list[list[str]]   # dnf: ["dnf","-y","install","moby-engine","docker-compose","docker-buildx"]; pacman: [..., "docker","docker-compose","docker-buildx"]

RunWithInput = Callable[[list[str], str], subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]]
class SudoSession:
    """Asked once. `sudo -S -p ''` reads the password from stdin; every step gets it that way."""
    def __init__(self, ask: runner.Prompter, run_input: RunWithInput, *, attempts: int = 3) -> None: ...
    def argv(self, cmd: list[str]) -> list[str]: ...        # ["sudo", "-S", "-p", "", *cmd]
    def run(self, cmd: list[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]: ...   # feeds password + "\n"
    def verify(self) -> bool: ...                            # `sudo -S -p '' -v`; asks up to `attempts` times; False when declined/wrong
    asked: int                                               # how many times ask() was called (test seam)
def _run_steps(do: RunCmd, commands: list[list[str]], *, sudo: bool, dry_run: bool,
               session: SudoSession | None = None) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]: ...
    # a step whose `sudo -n` stderr contains "password is required" and session is not None → session.verify() once, then session.run(cmd) for it and every later step
def _ensure_docker_linux(..., ask=None, run_input: RunWithInput | None = None) -> ProvisionReport: ...  # builds SudoSession(ask, run_input) when ask is given
def ensure_docker(..., run_input: RunWithInput | None = None) -> ProvisionReport: ...
```

### `yulon/catalog/preflight.py`
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Facts:            # existing fields plus:
    selinux_enforcing: bool | None = None
    server_fs_type: str | None = None
def gather(..., selinux: Callable[[], bool | None] = platform.selinux_enforcing,
           fs_type: Callable[[Path], str | None] = platform.filesystem_type) -> Facts: ...
# evaluate() adds Check("SELinux", "pass"|"warn"|"unchecked", ...): pass when not enforcing or labels supported;
# warn when enforcing and server_fs_type in SELINUX_NOLABEL_FS (":z" omitted; the daemon may refuse to
# create containers on this drive); unchecked when selinux_enforcing is None on linux.
```

## 7.2 — delete the bash lineage
- Delete: `catalog/installers/*/install-*.sh` (6), `wow-wotlk/dml-start.sh`, `wow-wotlk/wow-manage.sh`;
  `installer.Installer`, `PromptRule`, `PROMPT_RULES`, `ASK_THE_USER`, `make_responder`,
  `bash_available`, `NO_BASH_HELP`, `host_package_manager`, `platform_names`/`unsupported_platform_message`
  STAY (native uses them); `cancelled_install_message` STAYS (catalog_view uses it) but its script branch text is rewritten;
  `Install.script`, `script_platforms`, `script_variants`, `scripted_platforms()`, `uses_script()`, `script_for()`;
  `Install.is_native()` becomes `supports()` only; `Install.platforms` loses `min_length=1`;
  `tests/test_installer.py` script tests (keep dispatch/options tests), `tests/test_prompt.py` unchanged (the widget stays).
- Add: `catalog/installers/steam-deck/setup-gaming-mode.sh` (args: server dir, game id, ready regex).
- catalog.json: wow-tbc/vanilla/tortoise `platforms: []`, no `script`.
- `build/pylauncher.spec` datas: drop the scripts; keep `catalog/installers/**` templates + the steam-deck script.
- `pyplan/contribution.md` harness paragraph; style-guide §3 rows for `catalog/installer.py`, `catalog/catalog.py`.

## 7.3 — CMaNGOS family (data model, stage kinds, engine)

### `yulon/catalog/catalog.py` (7.3 additions)
```python
class Source(...)  # in yulon/manifest.py: rev: str | None = None  (commit pin; CloneSpec gains rev too; git.ContainerGit checks it out after clone)
MpqDepth = int | Literal["recursive"]
class ClientSpec(_Strict):
    required_file: str | None = None; min_mpq: int = 5; mpq_depth: MpqDepth = "recursive"
    locale_mpq_required: bool = False; near_client_warn_gb: float = 8.0
class DockerfileSpec(_Strict):
    make_jobs: int = 2
class ExtractTool(_Strict):
    name: str; argv: tuple[str, ...] = Field(min_length=1); produces: dict[str, int]   # dir under data/ → min files
class RetrySpec(_Strict):
    when_log_matches: str; tools: tuple[str, ...]                                  # tool names to re-run
class ExtractPlan(_Strict):
    image: str; tools: tuple[ExtractTool, ...] = Field(min_length=1); ulimit_stack_unlimited: bool = False
    retry: RetrySpec | None = None; stage_client: bool = False
class MmapPlan(_Strict):
    argv: tuple[str, ...]; min_files: int = 500; required: bool = True
class ConfPatch(_Strict):        # one file's patch table
    keys: dict[str, str]; match_commented: bool = False
class ConfPatchTable(_Strict):
    source_dir: str; files: dict[str, ConfPatch]
class SqlPhase(_Strict):
    name: str; into: str | None = None; into_each: dict[str, str] | None = None
    files: tuple[str, ...] = (); statements: tuple[str, ...] = (); gzip: bool = False
    sort: Literal["natural", "name"] = "natural"; on_error: Literal["fail", "warn"] = "fail"
class VerifyRule(_Strict):
    db: str; query: str; min: int
class PlayerData(_Strict):
    db: str; table: str; exclude_usernames: tuple[str, ...] = ()
class SqlPlan(_Strict):
    create: tuple[str, ...] = (); phases: tuple[SqlPhase, ...]; verify: tuple[VerifyRule, ...] = ()
    player_data: tuple[PlayerData, ...] = (); marker_db: str
    def plan_hash(self) -> str: ...          # sha256 of the canonical JSON, 16 hex
class CmangosData(_Strict):
    client: ClientSpec; dockerfile: DockerfileSpec; extract: ExtractPlan; mmaps: MmapPlan
    conf: ConfPatchTable; sql: SqlPlan
```

### `yulon/docker.py` (7.3 additions)
```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Mount:
    host: Path; guest: str; read_only: bool = False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ContainerRun:
    image: str; argv: tuple[str, ...]; mounts: tuple[Mount, ...] = (); workdir: str | None = None
    env: Mapping[str, str] = field(default_factory=dict); user_args: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    ulimits: tuple[str, ...] = ()            # e.g. ("stack=-1",) → --ulimit stack=-1
    def to_argv(self) -> list[str]: ...      # ["run", "--rm", ...] — pure, tested by field
def run_container(spec: ContainerRun, *, sink: OutputSink, cancel: threading.Event | None = None) -> AttachedRun: ...
def copy_from_image(image: str, src: str, dest: Path) -> None: ...     # create + cp + rm; raises DockerCommandError
def exec_stdin(container: str, argv: Sequence[str], source: BinaryIO, *, env: Mapping[str, str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]: ...
def sql_query(container: str, client: str, password: str, schema: str | None, statement: str) -> str: ...  # batch, skip-column-names, via exec; MYSQL_PWD env
```

### `yulon/catalog/families/` (7.3)
```python
# clientdir.py
def validate(client_dir: Path | None, spec: ClientSpec, *, free_bytes: Callable[[Path], int | None]) -> tuple[preflight.Check, ...]: ...
# dockerfile.py
def render(template_dir: Path, tokens: Mapping[str, str]) -> tuple[str, str]: ...        # (Dockerfile text, .dockerignore text), both starting with GENERATED_MARKER as a `#` comment
def write(server_dir: Path, dockerfile: str, dockerignore: str) -> tuple[Path, ...]: ...  # marker rule; refuses unmarked
# extract.py
EVIDENCE_FILE = ".yulon-extract.json"           # under data/
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ToolRecord: name: str; argv_hash: str; finished_unix: int
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Evidence: plan_hash: str; client_path: str; required_file_size: int | None; required_file_mtime: int | None; tools: tuple[ToolRecord, ...]
def read_evidence(data_dir: Path) -> Evidence | None: ...
def write_evidence(data_dir: Path, evidence: Evidence) -> None: ...
def tool_satisfied(tool: ExtractTool, data_dir: Path, evidence: Evidence | None, expected: Evidence) -> bool: ...
def run_plan(plan: ExtractPlan, *, image_ref: str, client_dir: Path, data_dir: Path, run_container: ..., user_args: Sequence[str], sink: OutputSink, cancel) -> Iterator[str]: ...
def run_mmaps(plan: MmapPlan, *, image_ref, data_dir, run_container, user_args, sink, cancel) -> Iterator[str]: ...
# conf.py
def patch(text: str, patch: ConfPatch, tokens: Mapping[str, str]) -> str: ...            # pure
```

## Amendments (2026-08-26, after the first draft and the consistency check) — BINDING, override anything above

A1. **Dispatch in 7.1.** `installer_for()` returns `Installer` when `entry.install.native is None`, else `family_for(entry)(...)`. `Install.scripted_platforms()`/`uses_script()`/`is_native()` bodies are NOT changed in 7.1 (they are deleted in 7.2). wow-wotlk's Linux flip is therefore A.3's dispatch change; B.7 only edits catalog.json (drop `script_platforms`) and its tests. In 7.2 `installer_for()` raises `InstallerError` for `native is None`; `install_wiring.main()` catches `InstallerError` around `installer_for_app()` and exits 1.
A2. **`AzerothCoreData` holds only `world_env`.** `AzerothCoreInstaller._import` uses `entry.containers.db_import` (and `_client_data` uses `entry.containers.client_data`).
A3. **`native.INSTALL_REALM_HOST = "127.0.0.1"`** — public module constant in `native.py`; the spine fills `{{REALM_HOST}}` from it; `families/cmangos.py` imports it.
A4. **`Stage.cancel_note` is yielded by the spine** immediately after `--- <name>` when non-empty; stage bodies do not yield their own note (the exception text still carries it through `_check_run`).
A5. **`ReadyMarkers.regex: bool = False`.** When False the spine `re.escape`s the filled `world`/`auth`/`fatal` strings before building `ReadySpec`; Tortoise sets `"regex": true` for its alternations. `docker.azerothcore_ready()` keeps escaping.
A6. **`composegen.fill(text: str, tokens: Mapping[str, str]) -> str`** is PUBLIC (the old `_fill` becomes an alias kept for the existing tests); every module fills through it. **`composegen.entry_tokens(entry: CatalogEntry) -> dict[str, str]`** is PUBLIC (added by group G): the CMaNGOS set `DB_IMAGE DB_HOST DB_USER AUTH_DB WORLD_DB CHAR_DB LOGS_DB CONTAINER_PREFIX CORE_DIR CLIENT_BUILD MAKE_JOBS` (`LOGS_DB` only when `databases.extra` is non-empty — omit the key otherwise, never `""`). `CORE_DIR` = the in-image install prefix = `PurePosixPath(native.cmangos.conf.source_dir).parent` (`/opt/mangos`, `/opt/tortoise`). `CmangosInstaller._tokens(ctx)` = `entry_tokens(entry)` + `DB_PASSWORD` (from `ctx.secrets`) + `REALM_HOST` (`INSTALL_REALM_HOST`) + `PROJECT_NAME IMAGE_PREFIX IMAGE_TAG DB_PORT AUTH_PORT WORLD_PORT` — one mapping, built once.
A7. **`stage_import(ctx, gate, service)`** ALWAYS runs the five-branch probe table first (skip on `imported` / complete `populated`; refuse `unreadable` / incomplete `populated`; `partial` → `gate.reset()` then continue). When `service` is None it RETURNS after the branch table (the family applies the SQL itself and re-probes/writes the marker); when a service is given it runs the one-shot + `verify_import` as today. `stage_start_db` is unconditional (no `db_import` gate); the AC family may short-circuit in its own wrapper.
A8. **Secrets.** `resolve_secrets()` mints `f"{prefix}{secrets.token_hex(8)}"`; `.db_password` is written with a trailing newline and read with `.strip()`. Stage-tuple construction errors raise `ValueError` (a family bug, not a user error).
A9. **Preflight threads the client dir:** `StagedInstaller._preflight_lines()` calls `self._seams.gather(entry, server_dir, client_dir=options.client_dir, platform_id=..., docker_ready=...)` from 7.1 (A.3); `gather()` accepts `client_dir: Path | None = None` from 7.1 and ignores it until 7.3 adds `client_validate`.
A10. **`sqlplan.expand(plan, server_dir, schemas, tokens)`** fills every `statement` through `composegen.fill` (never a file). `schemas` is the identity mapping `{name: name for name in (db.auth, db.characters, db.world, *db.extra)}`, built by `CmangosInstaller._schemas()`. **`sqlplan.verify(plan, *, container, client, password, sql_query) -> tuple[str, ...]`** (failing rule names) is public; `_import` calls `verify()` then `write_marker()`; the family has no `_verify`. `apply()` yields warn lines with the path RELATIVE TO THE SERVER DIR (store `rel: str` on `PhaseRun`). Cancel notes: `extract.EXTRACT_CANCEL_NOTE`, `extract.MMAPS_CANCEL_NOTE`, `native.IMPORT_CANCEL_NOTE` — imported, never redefined.
A11. **`docker.exec_stdin(container, argv, source, *, env)`** passes each env key as a bare `-e KEY` and the VALUE through the subprocess environment (never `-e KEY=value` in argv). `sql_query()` runs as root. `ContainerRun.env` values DO land in argv (`-e K=V`) — never put a secret there.
A12. **`extract.run_plan(..., required_file: str | None = None, client_build: int | None = None)`** — defaulted keyword-only; `CmangosInstaller._extract` passes both. `run_mmaps` refuses when no extraction evidence exists.
A13. **`Source.rev`** is a full 40-hex SHA (`pattern=r"^[0-9a-f]{40}$"`). `CloneSpec.rev` too; all three `CloneSpec(...)` constructions (spine + both AC bodies) pass `rev=source.rev`.
A14. **`NativeInstall.dockerfile_dir`** is defined ONCE, in 7.1 (B.3). G.2 adds only `cmangos` and the exactly-one-family-block validator; B.6's `generated_entry()` gains a minimal `cmangos=` block in G.2.
A15. **7.2 contract corrections:** `tests/test_prompt.py`'s six `Installer`-bound tests go; the widget and `runner.interact()` transport tests stay. `bash_available()` moves verbatim to `tests/support_bash.py`. F.4 also updates `test_the_three_cmangos_entries_have_no_native_block_yet` (`platforms == ()`), deletes `test_wotlk_names_no_script_platform_but_still_ships_its_scripts_until_7_2`, and F.3 removes `--reinstall` from nothing — `install_wiring.main()` never had it (D.3 must not add it). `catalog_view.py`'s "exited 0 with no docker-compose.yml" copy is rewritten in F.3.
A16. **Byte snapshot.** B.6 commits `tests/data/wotlk-rendered/{docker-compose.yml,docker-compose.override.yml,docker-compose.build.yml}` rendered with `bind_label=""` for a fixed `server_dir` (`/home/pk/wow-server-playerbots`, platform `linux`) and a test asserting `render()` reproduces them byte for byte; G.5/G.7 keep that test green (the WotLK templates must not change in 7.3).
A17. **Docs per gate.** E.5 owns every style-guide §3 row for 7.1 (`install_wiring.py`, `families/__init__.py`, `families/azerothcore.py`, the `native.py` row wording) and the `pylauncher/README.md` capability-table/"Installing a server" edits that E.3/E.4's gate records trigger; A.3 adds NO style-guide rows. pyyaml goes in `requirements-dev.txt` (test-only). The yulon-ubuntu alias is `ssh yulon-ubuntu`; box mutations in E.3 (purging Docker, making sudo ask) are steps the owner approves first, said so in the task.

Append to `tests/test_git.py`:

```python
# -- commit pins -------------------------------------------------------------

PIN = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567"


def test_a_pinned_source_is_fetched_by_hash_and_checked_out_detached(
    seen: list[list[str]], tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    """`rev` is honoured AFTER the clone, by hash, and never as a branch.

    A `--depth 1` clone holds only the tip, so `git checkout <sha>` on it answers
    "reference is not a tree" for every commit but one; the object has to be
    fetched first, and the fetch keeps the clone's own depth. `--detach`
    because a pin is not a branch: the next update must not drag it to the tip.
    """
    git.RunnerGit().clone(
        git.CloneSpec(url="https://example/repo.git", dest=tmp_path / "core", rev=PIN)
    )
    fetch = next(argv for argv in seen if "fetch" in argv)
    assert fetch[fetch.index("fetch") :] == ["fetch", "--depth=1", "origin", PIN]
    checkout = next(argv for argv in seen if "checkout" in argv)
    assert checkout[-3:] == ["checkout", "--detach", PIN]
    assert seen.index(fetch) > 0, "the clone comes first; the pin is applied to it"
    assert seen.index(fetch) < seen.index(checkout)


def test_an_unpinned_source_never_checks_anything_out(
    seen: list[list[str]], tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    git.RunnerGit().clone(git.CloneSpec(url="https://example/repo.git", dest=tmp_path / "core"))
    assert not any("checkout" in argv for argv in seen)


def test_updating_a_pinned_clone_re_applies_the_pin(
    seen: list[list[str]], tmp_path: Path
) -> None:
    """The update path resets to FETCH_HEAD (the tip); the pin must win afterwards."""
    dest = tmp_path / "core"
    (dest / ".git").mkdir(parents=True)
    git.RunnerGit().clone(git.CloneSpec(url="https://example/repo.git", dest=dest, rev=PIN))
    reset = next(index for index, argv in enumerate(seen) if "reset" in argv)
    checkout = next(index for index, argv in enumerate(seen) if "checkout" in argv)
    assert reset < checkout
    assert seen[checkout][-3:] == ["checkout", "--detach", PIN]


def test_container_git_pins_the_same_way(seen: list[list[str]], tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Two implementations of one Protocol must not disagree about what they produce."""
    dest = tmp_path / "core"
    git.ContainerGit().clone(
        git.CloneSpec(url="https://example/core.git", dest=dest, depth=None, rev=PIN)
    )
    fetch = next(argv for argv in seen if "fetch" in argv)
    assert fetch[fetch.index("fetch") :] == ["fetch", "origin", PIN], "depth=None → no --depth"
    checkout = next(argv for argv in seen if "checkout" in argv)
    assert checkout[:3] == ["docker", "run", "--rm"], "still containerised"
    assert checkout[-3:] == ["checkout", "--detach", PIN]
```

```python
def test_a_source_may_pin_a_full_commit_sha_and_nothing_else() -> None:
    """A pin is a SHA, not a ref: a tag can be moved, and GitHub serves a fetch by hash
    only for the full 40-hex object id (`uploadpack.allowReachableSHA1InWant`)."""
    from yulon.manifest import Source

    assert Source(repo="a/b").rev is None
    assert Source(repo="a/b", rev="0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567").rev is not None
    for bad in ("v1.0", "main", "0123456", "0123456789ABCDEF0123456789ABCDEF01234567"):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            Source(repo="a/b", rev=bad)
```

`yulon/git.py` — `CloneSpec` (grep for `class CloneSpec`) becomes:

```python
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CloneSpec:
    """One source to materialize at `dest`.

    Attributes:
        url: The repository to clone.
        dest: Where the working tree should end up.
        branch: Branch or tag to check out; None means the remote's default.
        sparse_path: Check out only this subdirectory (used for the guide/keg
            repos, where one directory out of a large tree is wanted).
        depth: Shallow-clone depth, or None for a full clone. Defaults to 1
            because most sources are content-only; AzerothCore's core repo must
            pass None, since its CMake reads the revision out of git metadata
            and a shallow clone gives it the wrong answer.
        rev: Full commit SHA to check out after the clone (and after every
            update), or None for the tip of `branch`. A pin for cores whose
            upstream moves under a gate — Tortoise is pinned the day 7.6 passes.
    """

    url: str
    dest: Path
    branch: str | None = None
    sparse_path: str | None = None
    depth: int | None = 1
    rev: str | None = None
```

`RunnerGit.clone` (grep for `def clone` inside `class RunnerGit`) becomes, and `_pin` is added right after `_update` (grep for `def _update`):

```python
    def clone(self, spec: CloneSpec) -> None:
        if (spec.dest / ".git").is_dir():
            self._update(spec)
            self._pin(spec)
            return
        if spec.dest.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(spec.dest)  # a non-git leftover; the old wow-manage.sh did the same
        spec.dest.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        if spec.sparse_path is None:
            argv = [
                "git",
                *_LINE_ENDING_ARGS,
                *_HTTP_VERSION_ARGS,
                "clone",
                *_LINE_ENDING_CONFIG,
                *_HTTP_VERSION_CONFIG,
                *_depth_args(spec.depth),
            ]
            if spec.branch:
                argv += ["--branch", spec.branch]
            _run_git([*argv, spec.url, str(spec.dest)])
        else:
            self._sparse_clone(spec)
        self._pin(spec)

    def _pin(self, spec: CloneSpec) -> None:
        """Move the checkout to `spec.rev` when the source pins one; a no-op otherwise.

        Fetched BY HASH first, because a shallow clone holds only the tip: `git
        checkout <sha>` on a `--depth 1` clone answers "reference is not a tree"
        for every commit but one. The fetch carries the clone's own depth so a
        shallow clone stays the shape it was created with (see `_update()`).
        `--detach` because a pin is not a branch: there is nothing to pull, and
        an attached HEAD would let the next `reset --hard FETCH_HEAD` drag the
        checkout back to the tip — which is why `clone()` re-applies the pin
        after every update as well as after the first clone.
        """
        if spec.rev is None:
            return
        _run_git(
            [
                "git",
                *_LINE_ENDING_ARGS,
                *_HTTP_VERSION_ARGS,
                "fetch",
                *_pull_depth_args(spec.depth),
                "origin",
                spec.rev,
            ],
            cwd=spec.dest,
        )
        _run_git(["git", *_LINE_ENDING_ARGS, "checkout", "--detach", spec.rev], cwd=spec.dest)
```

`ContainerGit.clone` (grep for `def clone` inside `class ContainerGit`) becomes, with `_pin` added before `_run` (grep for `def _run(self, spec: CloneSpec`):

```python
    def clone(self, spec: CloneSpec) -> None:
        if (spec.dest / ".git").is_dir():
            self._run(
                spec, ["fetch", *_pull_depth_args(spec.depth), "origin", spec.branch or "HEAD"]
            )
            self._run(spec, ["reset", "--hard", "FETCH_HEAD"])
            self._pin(spec)
            return
        if spec.dest.exists():
            shutil.rmtree(spec.dest)
        spec.dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        argv = [
            "clone",
            *_LINE_ENDING_CONFIG,
            *_HTTP_VERSION_CONFIG,
            *_depth_args(spec.depth),
        ]
        if spec.branch:
            argv += ["--branch", spec.branch]
        if spec.sparse_path is not None:
            argv += ["--filter=blob:none", "--sparse"]
        # The clone target is `.` because the mount point *is* the destination.
        self._run(spec, [*argv, spec.url, "."])
        if spec.sparse_path is not None:
            # --no-cone, or this checks out a DIFFERENT tree than RunnerGit.
            # `clone --sparse` turns cone mode on, and cone mode materializes
            # every file at the repo root and directly inside each parent
            # directory of the requested path. Measured on a repo with
            # ROOT.md, entrypoint.sh, guides/GUIDE.md and guides/x/a.txt,
            # sparse_path="guides/x": RunnerGit yields exactly guides/x/a.txt,
            # cone mode yields all four. Two implementations of one Protocol
            # must not disagree about what they produce.
            self._run(spec, ["sparse-checkout", "set", "--no-cone", spec.sparse_path.rstrip("/")])
        self._pin(spec)

    def _pin(self, spec: CloneSpec) -> None:
        """`RunnerGit._pin()`, containerised: fetch by hash at the clone's depth, then detach."""
        if spec.rev is None:
            return
        self._run(spec, ["fetch", *_pull_depth_args(spec.depth), "origin", spec.rev])
        self._run(spec, ["checkout", "--detach", spec.rev])
```

```python
def test_the_plan_hash_is_canonical_and_moves_with_the_plan() -> None:
    """The import marker records it; a reordered JSON file must not read as a new plan,
    a changed file list must."""
    plan = SqlPlan.model_validate(SQL_PLAN)
    again = SqlPlan.model_validate({**SQL_PLAN, "marker_db": "mangos", "create": ["mangos", "realmd"]})
    assert plan.plan_hash() == again.plan_hash()
    assert len(plan.plan_hash()) == 16 and int(plan.plan_hash(), 16) >= 0
    changed = SqlPlan.model_validate({**SQL_PLAN, "create": ["mangos"]})
    assert changed.plan_hash() != plan.plan_hash()

    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
```

Insert the following block after `Emulator` (grep for `class Emulator`; after 7.1's `EmulatorSource`/`Emulator`) and before `NativeInstall`:

```python
MpqDepth = int | Literal["recursive"]
"""How deep under `Data/` the MPQ count looks: a `find -maxdepth` value, or everywhere.

TBC's script searched with no depth limit, Vanilla's with `-maxdepth 1`, Tortoise's with
`-maxdepth 2` — three scripts, three numbers, so it is data (roadmap 7.3)."""


class ClientSpec(_Strict):
    """What the user's client folder must look like before an install may read it.

    Refusals and warnings only — `families/clientdir.py` turns these into preflight
    checks, never into a "Continue anyway?" prompt, because the engine cannot ask.
    """

    required_file: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        description=(
            "A file that proves the expansion, relative to the client dir (`Data/expansion.MPQ` "
            "for TBC, `Data/dbc.MPQ` for Vanilla). None disables this one rule — Tortoise's "
            "7272 client has no single defining file — while `Data/` and the MPQ count still apply."
        ),
    )
    min_mpq: int = Field(default=5, ge=1, description="Fewer MPQs than this is a WARNING.")
    mpq_depth: MpqDepth = "recursive"
    locale_mpq_required: bool = Field(
        default=False,
        description="TBC keeps its DBC data in `Data/<locale>/*.MPQ`; none at depth 2 is a warning.",
    )
    near_client_warn_gb: float = Field(
        default=8.0,
        gt=0,
        description="Warn when the client's volume has less free space than this (extraction scratch).",
    )

    @field_validator("mpq_depth")
    @classmethod
    def _depth_is_positive(cls, value: MpqDepth) -> MpqDepth:
        if isinstance(value, int) and value < 1:
            raise ValueError("mpq_depth must be >= 1 or 'recursive'")
        return value


class DockerfileSpec(_Strict):
    """Tokens the per-game `Dockerfile.tmpl` takes from data."""

    make_jobs: int = Field(
        default=2,
        ge=1,
        description=(
            "`make -j`. 2 is the scripts' number, chosen for a 16 GB Steam Deck: 2 GB per "
            "compiler job was measured on AzerothCore, and an OOM-killed gcc presents as "
            "'dies at the same % every retry'."
        ),
    )


class ExtractTool(_Strict):
    """One extractor run: its argv inside the image and what it must leave under `data/`."""

    name: str = Field(min_length=1)
    argv: tuple[str, ...] = Field(min_length=1)
    produces: dict[str, int] = Field(
        min_length=1,
        description="Directory under `data/` → minimum file count that means the tool finished.",
    )

    @field_validator("produces")
    @classmethod
    def _counts_are_positive(cls, value: dict[str, int]) -> dict[str, int]:
        for directory, count in value.items():
            if count < 1:
                raise ValueError(f"produces[{directory!r}] must be >= 1")
        return value


class RetrySpec(_Strict):
    """Re-run named tools once when their log matches (Vanilla's vmap extractor segfaults)."""

    when_log_matches: str = Field(min_length=1)
    tools: tuple[str, ...] = Field(min_length=1)


class ExtractPlan(_Strict):
    """The extraction stage as data: which image's tools, in which order, with what evidence."""

    image: str = Field(min_length=1, description="A key of `NativeInstall.images`.")
    tools: tuple[ExtractTool, ...] = Field(min_length=1)
    ulimit_stack_unlimited: bool = Field(
        default=False, description="`--ulimit stack=-1`; Vanilla's vmap tools need it."
    )
    retry: RetrySpec | None = None
    stage_client: bool = Field(
        default=False,
        description=(
            "Fallback if a tool insists on writing beside the client: lay a symlink farm "
            "(`cp -rs /client /work`) on a tmpfs and run there. Still no writes into the client."
        ),
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _retry_names_real_tools(self) -> ExtractPlan:
        if self.retry is not None:
            known = {tool.name for tool in self.tools}
            unknown = sorted(set(self.retry.tools) - known)
            if unknown:
                raise ValueError(f"retry names tools that do not exist: {unknown}")
        return self


class MmapPlan(_Strict):
    """The movement-map generator: its argv, and whether a shortfall refuses or warns."""

    argv: tuple[str, ...] = Field(min_length=1)
    min_files: int = Field(default=500, ge=1)
    required: bool = Field(
        default=True,
        description="False (Tortoise) turns a shortfall into a warning: bots need mmaps, a solo realm does not.",
    )


class ConfPatch(_Strict):
    """One conf file's `Key = value` table; values take the `{{TOKEN}}` grammar."""

    keys: dict[str, str] = Field(min_length=1)
    match_commented: bool = Field(
        default=False,
        description=(
            "Also rewrite a `# Key = ...` line. The Vanilla `AiPlayerbot.SyncLevel*` seds relied "
            "on this; everywhere else a commented key is left alone and the value is appended."
        ),
    )


class ConfPatchTable(_Strict):
    """Where the `.conf.dist` files come from inside the image, and how each is patched."""

    source_dir: str = Field(min_length=1, description="Absolute in-image dir of the `.conf.dist` files.")
    files: dict[str, ConfPatch] = Field(min_length=1)

    @field_validator("source_dir")
    @classmethod
    def _absolute(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.startswith("/"):
            raise ValueError(f"source_dir must be an absolute in-image path, got {value!r}")
        return value


class SqlPhase(_Strict):
    """One ordered step of the import: files (globs, relative to the server dir) or statements.

    Exactly one of `files`/`statements`; at most one of `into`/`into_each` (neither is a
    schema-less run, e.g. Tortoise's own `create_databases.sql`). `into_each` maps a schema
    to ITS glob, so `files` has no meaning beside it and `statements` cannot be split per db.
    Statements take the `{{TOKEN}}` grammar and are filled by `sqlplan.expand()` (A10).
    """

    name: str = Field(min_length=1)
    into: str | None = None
    into_each: dict[str, str] | None = None
    files: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    statements: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    gzip: bool = False
    sort: Literal["natural", "name"] = "natural"
    on_error: Literal["fail", "warn"] = Field(
        default="fail",
        description="`warn` logs every failing file by name and continues — the scripts' `2>/dev/null`, made visible.",
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _one_source_one_target(self) -> SqlPhase:
        if self.into is not None and self.into_each is not None:
            raise ValueError(f"phase {self.name!r}: `into` and `into_each` are alternatives")
        if self.into_each is not None:
            if self.statements:
                raise ValueError(f"phase {self.name!r}: `into_each` takes globs, not `statements`")
            if self.files:
                raise ValueError(f"phase {self.name!r}: `into_each` carries its own globs; drop `files`")
            if not self.into_each:
                raise ValueError(f"phase {self.name!r}: `into_each` is empty")
            return self
        if bool(self.files) == bool(self.statements):
            raise ValueError(f"phase {self.name!r}: exactly one of `files` or `statements`")
        return self


class VerifyRule(_Strict):
    """A COUNT query that must reach `min` before the import marker may be written."""

    db: str = Field(min_length=1)
    query: str = Field(min_length=1)
    min: int = Field(ge=0)


class PlayerData(_Strict):
    """A table whose rows mean 'somebody's server' — the import probe refuses, never drops."""

    db: str = Field(min_length=1)
    table: str = Field(min_length=1)
    exclude_usernames: tuple[str, ...] = Field(
        default=(),
        description="Seeded accounts (ADMINISTRATOR, GAMEMASTER...) that do not count as players.",
    )


class SqlPlan(_Strict):
    """The whole import: schemas to create, ordered phases, verify rules, the marker's home."""

    create: tuple[str, ...] = Field(
        default=(),
        description="Schemas phase 0 creates with the app user and grants; empty when upstream's SQL does it.",
    )
    phases: tuple[SqlPhase, ...] = Field(min_length=1)
    verify: tuple[VerifyRule, ...] = ()
    player_data: tuple[PlayerData, ...] = ()
    marker_db: str = Field(min_length=1, description="Where `yulon_install` (the marker table) lives.")

    def plan_hash(self) -> str:
        """16 hex of sha256 over the canonical JSON of this plan.

        Recorded in the marker row. Canonical (sorted keys, no whitespace, JSON mode) so a
        reordered `catalog.json` is the same plan and an edited glob is a new one; and a
        DIFFERENT hash in a marker still reads `imported` — a finished import from an older
        plan is never `partial` (phase7-decisions, "Probe").
        """
        canonical = json.dumps(
            self.model_dump(mode="json"), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")
        )
        return hashlib.sha256(canonical.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()[:16]


class CmangosData(_Strict):
    """Everything the CMaNGOS family needs that differs per game (roadmap 7.3)."""

    client: ClientSpec
    dockerfile: DockerfileSpec
    extract: ExtractPlan
    mmaps: MmapPlan
    conf: ConfPatchTable
    sql: SqlPlan
```

In `NativeInstall` (7.1's shape; grep for `class NativeInstall`), `dockerfile_dir` is ALREADY there from B.3 — do not touch it. Add ONE field after `azerothcore` and the validator after `floors_gb()`:

```python
    cmangos: CmangosData | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def _exactly_the_family_block(self) -> NativeInstall:
        """`family` names exactly the typed block that is present — no more, no fewer.

        A `cmangos` block on an `azerothcore` entry is a typo that would otherwise be data
        nobody reads; a missing block is an engine that starts and fails at stage two.
        Also pins `extract.image` to a built image, so the extractors run from something
        the build overlay produces.
        """
        blocks = {"azerothcore": self.azerothcore, "cmangos": self.cmangos}
        present = sorted(name for name, block in blocks.items() if block is not None)
        if present != [self.family]:
            raise ValueError(
                f"family is {self.family!r} but the blocks present are {present}; "
                f"exactly the `{self.family}` block must be present"
            )
        if self.cmangos is not None and self.cmangos.extract.image not in self.images:
            raise ValueError(
                f"cmangos.extract.image {self.cmangos.extract.image!r} is not one of images "
                f"{list(self.images)}"
            )
        return self
```

Confirm by grep that `dockerfile_dir` appears ONCE as a field in `NativeInstall` (`git grep -n "dockerfile_dir:" -- yulon/catalog/catalog.py` prints one line).

Append to `tests/test_composegen.py`:

```python
def test_fill_is_the_one_public_substitution_and_refuses_a_leftover_token() -> None:
    """Conf values, SQL statements and ready strings share the templates' grammar.

    One `fill()` so a token that a template and a conf table disagree about cannot become
    a silent literal in either — an unfilled `{{X}}` is an error wherever it is.
    """
    assert composegen.fill("{{A}};{{B}}", {"A": "1", "B": "2"}) == "1;2"
    with pytest.raises(composegen.ComposeGenError, match="NOPE"):
        composegen.fill("{{A}} {{NOPE}}", {"A": "1"})
    assert composegen.fill("plain", {"A": "unused"}) == "plain"
    assert composegen._fill is composegen.fill, "the old private name is an alias, not a copy"


def test_entry_tokens_spell_the_entry_once(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """`CORE_DIR` is the in-image install prefix (parent of `conf.source_dir`), never a
    host path; `MAKE_JOBS` is the dockerfile's number; `LOGS_DB` is the first extra schema."""
    entry = generated_entry(tmp_path, SAFE_BASE)
    tokens = composegen.entry_tokens(entry)
    assert tokens["CORE_DIR"] == "/opt/mangos"
    assert tokens["MAKE_JOBS"] == "2"
    assert tokens["DB_HOST"] == entry.containers.db
    assert tokens["CONTAINER_PREFIX"] == "tbc-"
    assert tokens["LOGS_DB"] == entry.databases.extra[0]
    assert set(tokens) == {
        "DB_IMAGE", "DB_HOST", "DB_USER", "AUTH_DB", "WORLD_DB", "CHAR_DB", "LOGS_DB",
        "CONTAINER_PREFIX", "CORE_DIR", "CLIENT_BUILD", "MAKE_JOBS",
    }


def test_logs_db_is_omitted_not_blanked_without_an_extra_schema(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    entry = generated_entry(tmp_path, SAFE_BASE)
    no_logs = entry.model_copy(
        update={"databases": entry.databases.model_copy(update={"extra": ()})}
    )
    tokens = composegen.entry_tokens(no_logs)
    assert "LOGS_DB" not in tokens
    with pytest.raises(composegen.ComposeGenError, match="LOGS_DB"):
        composegen.fill("{{LOGS_DB}}", tokens)


def test_the_family_tokens_come_from_the_entry(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """DB host, user, schema names, container prefix and client build are catalog facts."""
    templates = tmp_path / "native"
    templates.mkdir()
    (templates / "base.yml.tmpl").write_text(
        "name: {{PROJECT_NAME}}\n"
        "db: {{DB_IMAGE}} {{DB_HOST}} {{DB_USER}}\n"
        "schemas: {{AUTH_DB}} {{WORLD_DB}} {{CHAR_DB}} {{LOGS_DB}}\n"
        "prefix: {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}db\n"
        "build: {{CLIENT_BUILD}}\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (templates / "override.yml.tmpl").write_text("services: {}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (templates / "build.yml.tmpl").write_text("services:\n", encoding="utf-8")
    plan = composegen.render(
        entry_with_templates("native"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        tmp_path / "wow",
        templates_root=tmp_path,
        platform_id=lambda: "linux",
    )
    native = ENTRY.install.native
    assert native is not None
    assert f"db: {native.db.image} {ENTRY.containers.db} {native.db.user}" in plan.base
    assert "schemas: acore_auth acore_world acore_characters acore_playerbots" in plan.base
    assert "prefix: ac-db" in plan.base, "the common prefix of the three container names"
    assert f"build: {ENTRY.client.build}" in plan.base


def test_tokens_a_family_does_not_have_stay_unfilled_and_therefore_loud(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """WotLK has no `cmangos` block, so `MAKE_JOBS`/`CORE_DIR` do not exist for it.

    Not filled with "" — a blank `make -j` or a blank mount path is a silent literal — but
    left for `fill()` to refuse.
    """
    templates = tmp_path / "native"
    templates.mkdir()
    (templates / "base.yml.tmpl").write_text("jobs: {{MAKE_JOBS}}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (templates / "override.yml.tmpl").write_text("services: {}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (templates / "build.yml.tmpl").write_text("services:\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(composegen.ComposeGenError, match="MAKE_JOBS"):
        composegen.render(
            entry_with_templates("native"),  # type: ignore[arg-type]
            tmp_path / "wow",
            templates_root=tmp_path,
            platform_id=lambda: "linux",
        )
```

`Expected: FAIL with "AttributeError: module 'yulon.catalog.composegen' has no attribute 'fill'"`, `"... has no attribute 'entry_tokens'"` and `"ComposeGenError: unfilled compose placeholder near: 'db: {{DB_IMAGE}}..."`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`yulon/catalog/composegen.py`. Change the `from pathlib import Path` import (grep for it) to `from pathlib import Path, PurePosixPath`; `import os` is already there. Replace the whole `def _fill(template: str, values: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:` function (grep for `def _fill`) with:

```python
def fill(text: str, tokens: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
    """`{{TOKEN}}` substitution where an unresolved token is an ERROR.

    The ONE grammar for compose templates, Dockerfile templates, conf values,
    SQL statements and ready markers (roadmap 7.3, A6): a token the family
    fills for a template is the token it fills for a conf line, and an unknown
    one is an error in both places rather than a literal that reaches
    mangosd.conf or a compose file (which compose accepts happily as a string
    and which fails somewhere else entirely).

    Raises:
        ComposeGenError: a `{{...}}` survives substitution.
    """
    out = text
    for key, value in tokens.items():
        out = out.replace("{{" + key + "}}", value)
    start = out.find("{{")
    if start >= 0:
        raise ComposeGenError(f"unfilled compose placeholder near: {out[start:start + 40]!r}")
    return out


_fill = fill
"""The pre-7.3 private name; the existing tests call it, so it stays as an alias."""


def _container_prefix(entry: CatalogEntry) -> str:
    """The part the three container names share: `ac-` for WotLK, `tbc-` for TBC.

    The shared CMaNGOS templates name services `{{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}db`,
    `..realmd`, `..mangosd` so they equal the entry's container names — the
    AzerothCore convention `docker.start_database()` relies on. Derived rather
    than declared, so the catalog cannot say two different things; the
    invariants test asserts the rendered names against `containers.*`.
    """
    names = [entry.containers.db, entry.containers.auth, entry.containers.world]
    return os.path.commonprefix(names)


def entry_tokens(entry: CatalogEntry) -> dict[str, str]:
    """The per-game tokens every template, conf value and SQL statement may use.

    Facts the entry already states, spelled once: `DB_HOST` is the DB container
    (the conf files reach it by that name, not the scripts' bare `db`), the
    schema names are `databases.*`, and `LOGS_DB` is the first `extra` schema
    — omitted, not blanked, when there is none, so a template that wants it
    fails in `fill()` instead of writing `;;` into a conf. `MAKE_JOBS` and
    `CORE_DIR` exist only for a `cmangos` block: `CORE_DIR` is the core's
    in-image install prefix, derived as the parent of `conf.source_dir`
    (`/opt/mangos/etc` → `/opt/mangos`), which is where the binaries, the
    `etc/` bind and the `data/` bind all hang. `CmangosInstaller._tokens()`
    adds the per-install ones (`DB_PASSWORD`, `REALM_HOST`, ports, project,
    image prefix and tag) on top of this mapping (A6).
    """
    native = entry.install.native
    if native is None:
        raise ComposeGenError(f"{entry.name} has no `install.native` block to take tokens from.")
    tokens = {
        "DB_IMAGE": native.db.image,
        "DB_HOST": entry.containers.db,
        "DB_USER": native.db.user,
        "AUTH_DB": entry.databases.auth,
        "WORLD_DB": entry.databases.world,
        "CHAR_DB": entry.databases.characters,
        "CONTAINER_PREFIX": _container_prefix(entry),
        "CLIENT_BUILD": str(entry.client.build),
    }
    if entry.databases.extra:
        tokens["LOGS_DB"] = entry.databases.extra[0]
    if native.cmangos is not None:
        tokens["MAKE_JOBS"] = str(native.cmangos.dockerfile.make_jobs)
        tokens["CORE_DIR"] = str(PurePosixPath(native.cmangos.conf.source_dir).parent)
    return tokens
```

In `render()` (grep for `def render`), leave every local as B.5/B.6 left it (`texts["base.yml.tmpl"]`, `generated`, the `BIND_LABEL`-carrying override fill, the `{"BUILD_CONTEXT": "."}` build fill). Two edits only:

1. The base token dict literal (grep for `"BIND_LABEL": bind_label,`) gets ONE more item as its last entry:

```python
        **entry_tokens(entry),
```

2. The three `_fill(` calls inside `render()` become `fill(` (the alias would work; the public name is the one every module uses from now on).

```json
            "client": {"required_file": "Data/dbc.MPQ", "min_mpq": 5, "mpq_depth": 1,
                       "locale_mpq_required": false, "near_client_warn_gb": 8},
            "dockerfile": {"make_jobs": 2},
            "extract": {
              "image": "server", "ulimit_stack_unlimited": true,
              "tools": [
                {"name": "dbc and maps", "argv": ["/opt/mangos/bin/tools/ad", "-i", "/client", "-o", "/out"],
                 "produces": {"dbc": 100, "maps": 100}},
                {"name": "vmap extract", "argv": ["/opt/mangos/bin/tools/vmap_extractor", "-d", "/client/Data"],
                 "produces": {"Buildings": 100}},
                {"name": "vmap assemble", "argv": ["/opt/mangos/bin/tools/vmap_assembler", "Buildings", "vmaps"],
                 "produces": {"vmaps": 100}}
              ],
              "retry": {"when_log_matches": "Segmentation fault|core dumped", "tools": ["vmap extract", "vmap assemble"]}
            },
            "mmaps": {"argv": ["/opt/mangos/bin/tools/MoveMapGen", "--silent", "--threads", "2"],
                      "min_files": 500, "required": true},
            "conf": {
              "source_dir": "/opt/mangos/etc",
              "files": {
                "mangosd.conf": {"keys": {
                  "LoginDatabaseInfo": "\"{{DB_HOST}};3306;{{DB_USER}};{{DB_PASSWORD}};{{AUTH_DB}}\"",
                  "WorldDatabaseInfo": "\"{{DB_HOST}};3306;{{DB_USER}};{{DB_PASSWORD}};{{WORLD_DB}}\"",
                  "CharacterDatabaseInfo": "\"{{DB_HOST}};3306;{{DB_USER}};{{DB_PASSWORD}};{{CHAR_DB}}\"",
                  "LogsDatabaseInfo": "\"{{DB_HOST}};3306;{{DB_USER}};{{DB_PASSWORD}};{{LOGS_DB}}\"",
                  "DataDir": "\"/opt/mangos/data\"",
                  "WorldServerPort": "{{WORLD_PORT}}"}},
                "realmd.conf": {"keys": {
                  "LoginDatabaseInfo": "\"{{DB_HOST}};3306;{{DB_USER}};{{DB_PASSWORD}};{{AUTH_DB}}\""}},
                "aiplayerbot.conf": {"match_commented": true, "keys": {
                  "AiPlayerbot.MinRandomBots": "600",
                  "AiPlayerbot.MaxRandomBots": "800",
                  "AiPlayerbot.RandomBotAccountCount": "200",
                  "AiPlayerbot.SyncLevelWithPlayers": "1",
                  "AiPlayerbot.SyncLevelMaxAbove": "5",
                  "AiPlayerbot.SyncLevelNoPlayer": "1"}},
                "ahbot.conf": {"keys": {
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Chance.Sell": "75",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Creature.Normal": "90, 100, 30, 40",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Creature.Rare": "0, 30, 1, 2",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Creature.Elite": "80, 90, 4, 6",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Creature.RareElite": "0, 15, 1, 2",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Creature.WorldBoss": "-10, 2, 1, 1",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Disenchant": "40, 50, 2, 3",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Fishing": "12, 18, 100, 150",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Gameobject": "50, 60, 30, 45",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Loot.Skinning": "12, 18, 200, 250",
                  "AuctionHouseBot.Items.Profession": "250, 300, 0, 50"}}
              }
            },
            "sql": {
              "create": ["mangos", "realmd", "characters", "logs"],
              "phases": [
                {"name": "realmd base", "into": "realmd", "files": ["src/mangos-classic/sql/base/realmd.sql"], "on_error": "fail"},
                {"name": "characters base", "into": "characters", "files": ["src/mangos-classic/sql/base/characters.sql"], "on_error": "fail"},
                {"name": "logs base", "into": "logs", "files": ["src/mangos-classic/sql/base/logs.sql"], "on_error": "fail"},
                {"name": "world content", "into": "mangos", "files": ["src/classic-db/Full_DB/ClassicDB_*.sql.gz"], "gzip": true, "on_error": "fail"},
                {"name": "content updates", "into": "mangos", "files": ["src/classic-db/Updates/*.sql"], "sort": "natural", "on_error": "warn"},
                {"name": "ACID", "into": "mangos", "files": ["src/classic-db/ACID/acid_classic.sql"], "on_error": "warn"},
                {"name": "core updates", "into_each": {"mangos": "src/mangos-classic/sql/updates/mangos/*.sql", "realmd": "src/mangos-classic/sql/updates/realmd/*.sql", "characters": "src/mangos-classic/sql/updates/characters/*.sql", "logs": "src/mangos-classic/sql/updates/logs/*.sql"}, "sort": "natural", "on_error": "warn"},
                {"name": "spell_template hotfix", "into": "mangos", "statements": ["ALTER TABLE spell_template ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS EffectBonusCoefficient1 FLOAT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0' AFTER RequiredAuraVision, ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS EffectBonusCoefficient2 FLOAT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0' AFTER EffectBonusCoefficient1, ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS EffectBonusCoefficient3 FLOAT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0' AFTER EffectBonusCoefficient2, ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS EffectBonusCoefficientFromAP1 FLOAT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0' AFTER EffectBonusCoefficient3, ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS EffectBonusCoefficientFromAP2 FLOAT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0' AFTER EffectBonusCoefficientFromAP1, ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS EffectBonusCoefficientFromAP3 FLOAT NOT NULL DEFAULT '0' AFTER EffectBonusCoefficientFromAP2"], "on_error": "warn"},
                {"name": "playerbots characters", "into": "characters", "files": ["src/mangos-classic/src/modules/Bots/sql/characters/*.sql"], "on_error": "warn"},
                {"name": "playerbots world", "into": "mangos", "files": ["src/mangos-classic/src/modules/Bots/sql/world/*.sql", "src/mangos-classic/src/modules/Bots/sql/world/classic/*.sql"], "on_error": "warn"}
              ],
              "verify": [
                {"db": "mangos", "query": "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM item_template", "min": 17000},
                {"db": "mangos", "query": "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.tables WHERE table_schema='mangos' AND table_name LIKE 'ai_playerbot%'", "min": 10}
              ],
              "player_data": [
                {"db": "characters", "table": "characters"},
                {"db": "realmd", "table": "account", "exclude_usernames": ["ADMINISTRATOR", "GAMEMASTER", "MODERATOR", "PLAYER"]}
              ],
              "marker_db": "mangos"
            }
          }
        }
      },
```


(Tortoise's script had mariadb create the `mangos` user through `MARIADB_USER`; the shared template sets no such variable — one template — so the grants phase creates the user itself. Every `{{TOKEN}}` in a statement is filled by `sqlplan.expand(plan, server_dir, schemas, tokens)` through `composegen.fill` (A10); `{{DB_PASSWORD}}` reaches the client on stdin through `exec_stdin`, never in argv (A11). `"regex": true` because the ready and fatal markers are alternations that the spine must hand to `ReadySpec` un-escaped (A5). Tortoise conf keys are the old install-tortoise-wow-wsl.sh's `mangosd.conf`/`realmd.conf` sed table verbatim — `git show 0165e226:pylauncher/catalog/installers/wow-tortoise/install-tortoise-wow-wsl.sh` and grep for `GM.LoginState`; the ready/fatal regexes are that script's `READY_RE`/`FATAL_RE`.)

### Task G.5: The shared CMaNGOS compose templates

**Files:**
- Create: `catalog/installers/shared/cmangos/base.yml.tmpl`
- Create: `catalog/installers/shared/cmangos/override.yml.tmpl`
- Create: `catalog/installers/shared/cmangos/build.yml.tmpl`
- Test: `tests/test_composegen.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: G.3's `render()` token set; G.4's entries; `composegen.GENERATED_MARKER`, `built_image_refs(entry, server_dir, ...)` (7.1); `keys_in()` (B.5's helper in `tests/test_composegen.py`)
- Produces: one template set that renders for all three CMaNGOS entries; services `<prefix>db`/`<prefix>realmd`/`<prefix>mangosd` equal to `containers.*`; DB port loopback-bound; `{{BIND_LABEL}}` on the three host-bind lines; the WotLK templates untouched (A16)

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Append to `tests/test_composegen.py`:

```python
CMANGOS_ENTRIES = [load_catalog().get(g) for g in ("wow-tbc", "wow-vanilla", "wow-tortoise")]


def service_names(compose_text: str) -> set[str]:
    """Keys directly under `services:` — and ONLY there.

    `networks:` and `volumes:` keys sit at the same two-space indent, so a whole-file
    regex would count `tbc-net` and `db-data` as services; the scan stops at the next
    top-level key.
    """
    names: set[str] = set()
    inside = False
    for line in compose_text.splitlines():
        if re.match(r"^services:\s*$", line):
            inside = True
            continue
        if inside and re.match(r"^\S", line):
            break
        if inside:
            match = re.match(r"^  ([A-Za-z0-9-]+):\s*$", line)
            if match:
                names.add(match.group(1))
    return names


def render_generated(entry: object, server_dir: Path) -> composegen.ComposePlan:
    """A generated-password entry needs the secret handed in; it never reaches the file."""
    return composegen.render(
        entry,  # type: ignore[arg-type]
        server_dir,
        templates_root=TEMPLATES,
        db_password="tbc-0123456789abcdef",
        bind_label=":z",
        platform_id=lambda: "linux",
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("entry", CMANGOS_ENTRIES, ids=lambda e: e.id)
def test_the_shared_cmangos_templates_render_for_every_family_entry(
    tmp_path: Path, entry: object
) -> None:
    plan = render_generated(entry, tmp_path / "wow")
    for text in (plan.base, plan.override, plan.build):
        assert text.startswith(composegen.GENERATED_MARKER)
        assert "{{" not in text
    assert "ports" in keys_in(plan.base)
    assert "ports" not in keys_in(plan.override)
    assert "ports" not in keys_in(plan.build)
    assert "build" not in keys_in(plan.base)


def test_the_cmangos_services_are_named_after_their_containers(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """`ContainerSpec.services` keeps its default, so `compose up -d --no-deps <db>` works."""
    entry = load_catalog().get("wow-tbc")
    plan = render_generated(entry, tmp_path / "wow")
    services = service_names(plan.base)
    assert services == {entry.containers.db, entry.containers.auth, entry.containers.world}
    for name in services:
        assert f"container_name: {name}" in plan.base


def test_the_cmangos_database_is_loopback_only_with_the_mariadb_healthcheck(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """Never the scripts' `3306:3306`; and the healthcheck is mariadb's own script."""
    entry = load_catalog().get("wow-tortoise")
    plan = render_generated(entry, tmp_path / "wow")
    assert f'"127.0.0.1:${{DOCKER_DB_EXTERNAL_PORT:-{entry.ports.db}}}:3306"' in plan.base
    assert '["CMD", "healthcheck.sh", "--connect", "--innodb_initialized"]' in plan.base
    assert "image: mariadb:10.6" in plan.base


def test_a_generated_password_never_reaches_the_compose_files(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    plan = render_generated(load_catalog().get("wow-tbc"), tmp_path / "wow")
    for text in (plan.base, plan.override, plan.build):
        assert "tbc-0123456789abcdef" not in text
    assert "${DB_ROOT_PASSWORD:?Yu'lon .env is missing}" in plan.base
    assert plan.dotenv == {"DB_ROOT_PASSWORD": "tbc-0123456789abcdef"}


def test_the_cmangos_world_server_keeps_its_console_and_shutdown_grace(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    plan = render_generated(load_catalog().get("wow-vanilla"), tmp_path / "wow")
    assert "stdin_open: true" in plan.base
    assert "stop_grace_period: 5m" in plan.base


def test_the_cmangos_build_overlay_builds_exactly_the_server_image(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    entry = load_catalog().get("wow-tbc")
    plan = render_generated(entry, tmp_path / "wow")
    assert plan.build.count("dockerfile: Dockerfile") == 1
    assert "context: ." in plan.build
    refs = composegen.built_image_refs(entry, tmp_path / "wow", platform_id=lambda: "linux")
    base_images = set(re.findall(r"^\s*image:\s*(\S+)\s*$", plan.base, re.MULTILINE))
    assert set(refs) <= base_images
    assert len(refs) == 1 and refs[0].startswith("yulon.local/cmangos-tbc-server:native-")


def test_the_cmangos_host_binds_carry_the_label_and_the_volume_does_not(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    plan = render_generated(load_catalog().get("wow-tbc"), tmp_path / "wow")
    binds = [line for line in plan.base.splitlines() if line.strip().startswith("- ./")]
    assert len(binds) == 3
    assert all(line.endswith(":z") for line in binds)
    assert "- db-data:/var/lib/mysql" in plan.base
    assert "/var/lib/mysql:z" not in plan.base
    assert "./etc:/opt/mangos/etc:z" in plan.base
    assert "./data:/opt/mangos/data:z" in plan.base
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation**

`catalog/installers/shared/cmangos/base.yml.tmpl` (the header comment spells `TOKEN` without braces on purpose — `fill()` refuses ANY surviving `{{`, comments included; every other `{{...}}` in a comment below is a real token and is filled):

```yaml
# generated by Yu'lon — do not hand-edit; the install engine rewrites this file.
#
# The CMaNGOS-lineage stack (WoW TBC, WoW Vanilla, WoW Tortoise) for the native
# install engine (roadmap 7.3). ONE template for the family: every per-game
# value is a TOKEN in double braces that composegen fills from catalog.json,
# and a token nobody fills is an error at generation time, never a literal in
# this file.
#
# THREE FILES, the same split as WotLK's and for the same reasons:
#   * docker-compose.yml        (this file) name:, services, container names,
#     image refs, ALL ports, binds, volumes, depends_on, healthchecks.
#   * docker-compose.override.yml   runtime settings only, auto-loaded.
#   * docker-compose.build.yml      build: blocks only, never auto-loaded, so a
#     post-install `up` can never start a multi-hour rebuild.
#
# SERVICES ARE NAMED AFTER THEIR CONTAINERS ({{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}db,
# {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}realmd, {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}mangosd) — the AzerothCore
# convention — so `ContainerSpec.services` keeps its default and
# `docker.start_database()`'s `compose up -d --no-deps <db>` works unchanged.
# The one price is that the emulator's conf files reach the database by the
# container name ({{DB_HOST}}), not the scripts' bare `db`.
#
# THE PASSWORD IS NEVER IN THIS FILE. The family generates one per install,
# keeps it in `.db_password` (0600) and hands it to compose through `.env` as
# DB_ROOT_PASSWORD; `${VAR:?message}` makes compose refuse to start without it
# rather than start a database with an empty root password. The invariants
# test asserts no password text survives into a generated-password template.
#
# THE IMAGE TAG IS WRITTEN IN, NOT INTERPOLATED, for the reason WotLK's base
# template gives at length: nothing writes IMAGE_TAG into .env, and a wrapper
# with no writer only lets a hand edit desynchronise the build from the
# resume check.
name: {{PROJECT_NAME}}

services:
  {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}db:
    container_name: {{DB_HOST}}
    image: {{DB_IMAGE}}
    networks: [{{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}net]
    ports:
      # Loopback ONLY, as WotLK's template does — every CMaNGOS script bound
      # 3306 on all interfaces, and nothing off this machine has business
      # reaching the database. The variable name is a contract with the
      # port-conflict remedy, which appends DOCKER_DB_EXTERNAL_PORT=13306 to
      # .env when 3306 is taken.
      - "127.0.0.1:${DOCKER_DB_EXTERNAL_PORT:-{{DB_PORT}}}:3306"
    environment:
      MARIADB_ROOT_PASSWORD: ${DB_ROOT_PASSWORD:?Yu'lon .env is missing}
    volumes:
      - db-data:/var/lib/mysql
    restart: unless-stopped
    healthcheck:
      # mariadb's own probe, shipped in the official 10.6 and 11 images: it
      # connects over TCP as the image's healthcheck user and waits for InnoDB
      # to finish recovery, which is the moment an import may stream. Proved on
      # mariadb:11 by the 7.3 primitives gate; the Tortoise script used it on
      # 10.6.
      test: ["CMD", "healthcheck.sh", "--connect", "--innodb_initialized"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 10s
      retries: 40
      start_period: 60s

  {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}realmd:
    container_name: {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}realmd
    image: {{IMAGE_PREFIX}}server:{{IMAGE_TAG}}
    networks: [{{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}net]
    # One image serves realmd, mangosd, the extractors and the conf source, as
    # it did in the scripts; the binaries sit in {{CORE_DIR}}/bin and read
    # their conf from {{CORE_DIR}}/etc, the compiled-in sysconfdir — which is
    # why ./etc is bound THERE and not somewhere a `-c` flag would have to name
    # (ahbot.conf and aiplayerbot.conf are found by that compiled-in path).
    working_dir: {{CORE_DIR}}/bin
    command: ["./realmd"]
    restart: unless-stopped
    ports:
      # The game client connects to this from the LAN, so it is deliberately
      # NOT loopback-pinned (unlike the DB port).
      - "${DOCKER_AUTH_EXTERNAL_PORT:-{{AUTH_PORT}}}:3724"
    volumes:
      - ./etc:{{CORE_DIR}}/etc{{BIND_LABEL}}
    depends_on:
      {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}db:
        condition: service_healthy

  {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}mangosd:
    container_name: {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}mangosd
    image: {{IMAGE_PREFIX}}server:{{IMAGE_TAG}}
    networks: [{{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}net]
    working_dir: {{CORE_DIR}}/bin
    command: ["./mangosd"]
    # stdin + tty keep the world console attachable (`docker attach
    # --sig-proxy=false` refuses a non-TTY stdin), the same reason WotLK's
    # worldserver carries both.
    stdin_open: true
    tty: true
    restart: unless-stopped
    # Inherited from WotLK's measured 5m (docker.STOP_GRACE_SECONDS), not
    # measured for a CMaNGOS realm: a populated bot realm drains character
    # saves on shutdown and compose's 10s default SIGKILLs that mid-save. The
    # first CMaNGOS gate owes a shutdown timing.
    stop_grace_period: 5m
    ports:
      # Container-side port equals the host port: the conf patch pins
      # WorldServerPort to the same {{WORLD_PORT}}, so one number is the truth
      # for the realmlist row, the conf and this mapping.
      - "${DOCKER_WORLD_EXTERNAL_PORT:-{{WORLD_PORT}}}:{{WORLD_PORT}}"
    volumes:
      # Both bound out to the host: ./etc is where the conf stage patches and
      # the user edits; ./data is what the extract and mmaps stages produce,
      # and what DataDir in mangosd.conf points at inside the container.
      - ./etc:{{CORE_DIR}}/etc{{BIND_LABEL}}
      - ./data:{{CORE_DIR}}/data{{BIND_LABEL}}
    depends_on:
      {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}db:
        condition: service_healthy

networks:
  {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}net:

volumes:
  db-data:
```

`catalog/installers/shared/cmangos/override.yml.tmpl`:

```yaml
# generated by Yu'lon — runtime settings for the native stack.
#
# THIS FILE BELONGS TO THE SETTINGS SURFACE, not to the install engine. Compose
# auto-loads it next to docker-compose.yml, and whatever writes settings next
# will read and rewrite it — a YAML writer keeps sibling keys but drops
# comments and restyles what it emits, so this header will not survive the
# first save from the app. That is exactly why nothing structural (services,
# ports, image refs, build config) may live here.
#
# The CMaNGOS family has no runtime environment to put here yet: mangosd reads
# its settings from the conf files under ./etc, which the conf stage patches
# from catalog data. The empty services mapping is what keeps this file a valid
# compose document until a settings surface has something to say.
#
# Do NOT add a ports: entry to this file. Compose CONCATENATES ports lists
# across files, so it would publish a SECOND binding rather than replacing the
# base one — and `test_composegen.py` fails the build if one appears.
services: {}
```

`catalog/installers/shared/cmangos/build.yml.tmpl`:

```yaml
# generated by Yu'lon — BUILD-ONLY overlay for the native install.
#
# NEVER auto-loaded. Compose picks up docker-compose.yml plus
# docker-compose.override.yml on its own; this file is merged in ONLY when the
# engine passes it explicitly:
#
#     docker compose -f docker-compose.yml -f docker-compose.override.yml \
#                    -f docker-compose.build.yml build
#
# so a post-install `up` can never start a multi-hour rebuild. Naming any -f at
# all disables auto-loading, which is why the other two files are listed too.
# `docker.build_staged()` is the only thing allowed to spell that command.
#
# ONE build block, on the world server: realmd shares the image by reference,
# so compose builds `{{IMAGE_PREFIX}}server` once and both services run it.
# The context is the server dir — the engine's Dockerfile there COPYs the
# ./src trees the clone stage laid down — and `dockerfile:` is spelled even
# though it is the default, because a missing key is how the Rust launcher's
# first real native build died.
services:
  {{CONTAINER_PREFIX}}mangosd:
    build:
      context: {{BUILD_CONTEXT}}
      dockerfile: Dockerfile
```

```python
@pytest.mark.parametrize("entry", CMANGOS_ENTRIES, ids=lambda e: e.id)
def test_every_cmangos_entry_has_a_dockerfile_pair_that_fills_from_its_tokens(entry: object) -> None:
    """Rendered through the one `fill()`; the marker is a `#` first line so the
    generated-file rule (`dockerfile.write()` refuses an unmarked file) has something to read."""
    native = entry.install.native  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    assert native is not None and native.dockerfile_dir is not None
    template_dir = TEMPLATES / native.dockerfile_dir
    tokens = composegen.entry_tokens(entry)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    for name in ("Dockerfile.tmpl", "dockerignore.tmpl"):
        text = composegen.fill((template_dir / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"), tokens)
        assert text.startswith(composegen.GENERATED_MARKER), name
        assert "{{" not in text
    dockerfile = composegen.fill(
        (template_dir / "Dockerfile.tmpl").read_text(encoding="utf-8"), tokens
    )
    assert f"make -j{native.cmangos.dockerfile.make_jobs}" in dockerfile
    assert "git clone" not in dockerfile, "sources are COPY'd from the clone stage, never fetched"
    core = entry.emulator.sources[0].dest  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    assert f"COPY {core} " in dockerfile
    assert f"CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX={tokens['CORE_DIR']}" in dockerfile
    assert tokens["CORE_DIR"].startswith("/opt/"), "the in-image prefix, never the host src dir"
    ignore = (template_dir / "dockerignore.tmpl").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert ".git" in ignore, "the .git tree is not build context"
```
