(c) Create `yulon/catalog/families/cmangos.py`:

```python
"""The CMaNGOS-lineage install engine: one class for every entry whose `family` is `cmangos`.

Stage ORDER is Python (this tuple); stage PARAMETERS are the entry's typed
`install.native.cmangos` block. The class therefore names no game — a test
asserts, over code with docstrings stripped, that no game literal appears —
and the third CMaNGOS game costs a catalog entry plus templates, no Python
(`pyplan/phase7-decisions.md`, "Family engines — chosen").

Every body here is a thin wrapper: it pulls one typed block off the entry,
resolves the few values only the engine knows (the built image reference, the
uid:gid policy, the token mapping, the secret) and hands them to a stage-kind
module (`dockerfile`, `extract`, `conf`, `sqlplan`). The spine
(`StagedInstaller`) owns clone, compose, build, start-db, up and ready, it
owns the import branch table, and it yields every stage's cancel note — this
family only supplies the `MarkerGate` and runs the SQL plan when the table
says run.

`db-password` is the one stage with a body of its own, because its evidence is
a file and not the state file: a state file must never be the thing that
claims a secret exists.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import queue
import threading
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from pathlib import Path
from typing import ClassVar

from yulon import docker, platform
from yulon.catalog import composegen
from yulon.catalog.catalog import CmangosData, NativeInstall
from yulon.catalog.installer import InstallerError
from yulon.catalog.native import (
    BUILD_CANCEL_NOTE,
    INSTALL_REALM_HOST,
    Stage,
    StageContext,
    StagedInstaller,
)
from yulon.log import get_logger

logger = get_logger(__name__)

DATA_DIR = "data"
"""Where extraction lands, relative to the server dir; the compose template binds it to /opt/*/data."""

ETC_DIR = "etc"
"""Where the patched `.conf` files live, relative to the server dir."""

DB_DATA_VOLUME = "db-data"
"""The named volume key in `catalog/installers/shared/cmangos/base.yml.tmpl`.

Compose names the volume `<project>_<key>`; `db-password` asks the daemon
about exactly that name. The catalog invariants test pins the template key to
this constant so the two cannot drift apart silently.
"""


class CmangosInstaller(StagedInstaller):
    """Install a CMaNGOS-lineage server: clone, Dockerfile, build, extract, conf, SQL plan, start."""

    family = "cmangos"
    STAGE_NAMES: ClassVar[tuple[str, ...]] = (
        "clone-sources",
        "db-password",
        "write-dockerfile",
        "generate-compose",
        "build",
        "extract",
        "mmaps",
        "conf",
        "start-db",
        "import",
        "up",
        "ready",
    )

    def stages(self) -> tuple[Stage, ...]:
        """The family's stage tuple, in `STAGE_NAMES` order."""
        return (
            Stage("clone-sources", self._clone_sources),
            Stage("generate-compose", self.stage_generate_compose),
            Stage("build", self.stage_build, cancel_note=BUILD_CANCEL_NOTE),
            Stage("start-db", self.stage_start_db, recorded=False),
            Stage("up", self.stage_up, recorded=False),
            Stage("ready", self.stage_ready, recorded=False),
        )

    # -- stage bodies ----------------------------------------------------

    def _clone_sources(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]:
        """Every source to its own `dest` — one stage, because no CMaNGOS state file predates it."""
        yield from self.stage_clone_sources(ctx, self.entry.emulator.sources)

    # -- what only the engine knows ---------------------------------------

    def _native(self) -> NativeInstall:
        native_block = self.entry.install.native
        if native_block is None:  # preflight refuses first; this keeps the type honest
            raise InstallerError(f"{self.entry.name} has no `install.native` section.")
        return native_block

    def _data(self) -> CmangosData:
        """The typed block every family stage reads; its absence is a catalog bug, not a machine's."""
        data = self._native().cmangos
        if data is None:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"{self.entry.name} says its family is cmangos but carries no `cmangos` block. "
                "That is a bug in the app's catalog, not something to fix on this machine."
            )
        return data

    def _tokens(self, ctx: StageContext) -> dict[str, str]:
        """The one token mapping for the Dockerfile, the conf tables, the SQL statements and verify.

        `composegen.entry_tokens()` is the catalog-derived set the compose
        templates use (`DB_IMAGE` … `MAKE_JOBS`, `CORE_DIR` being the in-image
        install prefix, `LOGS_DB` absent rather than empty when the entry has
        no extra schema); this adds what only an install knows. The same
        grammar and the same `composegen.fill()` everywhere — a value between
        a template and a conf table cannot become a silent literal, because an
        unknown token is refused. `DB_PASSWORD` is rendered here on purpose:
        conf files are written 0600 and the emulator reads them; the compose
        templates never see it in generated mode.
        """
        native_block = self._native()
        server_dir = ctx.server_dir
        platform_id = self._seams.platform_id
        return {
            **composegen.entry_tokens(self.entry),
            "PROJECT_NAME": composegen.project_name(
                self.entry.id, server_dir, platform_id=platform_id
            ),
            "IMAGE_PREFIX": native_block.image_prefix,
            "IMAGE_TAG": composegen.image_tag(server_dir, platform_id=platform_id),
            "DB_PORT": str(self.entry.ports.db),
            "AUTH_PORT": str(self.entry.ports.auth),
            "WORLD_PORT": str(self.entry.ports.world),
            "DB_PASSWORD": ctx.secrets.db_password,
            "REALM_HOST": INSTALL_REALM_HOST,
        }

    def _image_ref(self, ctx: StageContext, service: str) -> str:
        """The fully qualified reference of the image `service` names in `native.images`.

        The one image per CMaNGOS game is extractor, conf source and runtime at
        once; the data block names it by service key, and the reference is the
        same string `stage_build` asked the daemon about.
        """
        prefix = self._native().image_prefix
        refs = composegen.built_image_refs(
            self.entry, ctx.server_dir, platform_id=self._seams.platform_id
        )
        for ref in refs:
            if ref.startswith(f"{prefix}{service}:"):
                return ref
        raise InstallerError(
            f"{service} is not one of the images this install builds "
            f"({', '.join(self._native().images)}). That is a catalog error in the app, not "
            "something to fix on this machine."
        )

    def _user_args(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """`--user uid:gid` on Linux, nothing on Docker Desktop — one policy, `platform.py`'s."""
        return tuple(platform.container_user_args(platform_id=self._seams.platform_id))

    def _stream(self, call: Callable[[docker.OutputSink], Iterator[str]]) -> Iterator[str]:
        """Run a stage-kind generator that ALSO takes a sink, and yield both streams live.

        `extract.run_plan()` and `sqlplan.apply()` yield their own progress
        lines and push container output into a sink. The spine's `_pump()`
        bridges a push-only call; this is the same queue-and-worker bridge for a
        call that is both, so a two-hour extraction shows line by line rather
        than as a list at the end.
        """
        lines: queue.Queue[str | None] = queue.Queue()
        failure: list[BaseException] = []

        def work() -> None:
            try:
                for line in call(lines.put):
                    lines.put(line)
            except BaseException as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - re-raised on the caller's thread below
                failure.append(exc)
            finally:
                lines.put(None)

        worker = threading.Thread(target=work, daemon=True, name="yulon-cmangos-output")
        worker.start()
        while True:
            item = lines.get()
            if item is None:
                break
            yield item
        worker.join()
        if failure:
            exc = failure[0]
            if isinstance(exc, InstallerError):
                raise exc
            raise InstallerError(f"the step could not be run: {exc}") from exc
```

Note: `stages()` is completed by K.3–K.7 (each inserts its `Stage` in `STAGE_NAMES` order); `FAMILIES` registration happens in K.8 once the tuple is whole. The cancel notes are never defined here: `extract.EXTRACT_CANCEL_NOTE`, `extract.MMAPS_CANCEL_NOTE` and `native.IMPORT_CANCEL_NOTE` are imported where K.5/K.7 bind them (A10).

```python
# -- db-password --------------------------------------------------------------


def _db_volume(server_dir: Path) -> str:
    project = composegen.project_name(ENTRY.id, server_dir, platform_id=lambda: "linux")
    return f"{project}_db-data"


def test_db_password_writes_the_generated_secret_with_the_trailing_newline_the_spine_strips(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    server_dir.mkdir()
    rec = Recorder()
    eng = engine(rec)
    said = list(eng._db_password(context(server_dir)))
    assert ENTRY.install.password.file is not None
    secret = server_dir / ENTRY.install.password.file
    assert secret.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == DB_PASSWORD + "\n"
    assert any("readable only by you" in line for line in said)
    if os.name != "nt":
        assert (secret.stat().st_mode & 0o777) == 0o600
    # A second run sees the file and touches nothing — the volume is not even asked about.
    before = secret.stat().st_mtime_ns
    again = list(eng._db_password(context(server_dir)))
    assert secret.stat().st_mtime_ns == before
    assert any("already" in line for line in again)


def test_db_password_refuses_when_the_file_is_gone_but_the_volume_exists(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    """The volume was initialised with the password the file held; a new one locks us out."""
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    server_dir.mkdir()
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.volumes.add(_db_volume(server_dir))
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError) as refusal:
        list(engine(rec)._db_password(context(server_dir)))
    assert ".db_password" in str(refusal.value)
    assert _db_volume(server_dir) in str(refusal.value)
    assert not (server_dir / ".db_password").exists()


def test_db_password_refuses_when_docker_will_not_say_whether_the_volume_exists(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    server_dir.mkdir()

    def unanswerable(name: str) -> bool:
        raise docker.DockerCommandError("Cannot connect to the Docker daemon")

    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="cannot prove"):
        list(engine(Recorder(), volume_exists=unanswerable)._db_password(context(server_dir)))
    assert not (server_dir / ".db_password").exists()


def test_db_password_is_a_stage_that_is_never_recorded_and_follows_clone_sources() -> None:
    stage = next(s for s in engine(Recorder()).stages() if s.name == "db-password")
    assert stage.recorded is False
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — in `cmangos.py`, insert `Stage("db-password", self._db_password, recorded=False),` directly after the `clone-sources` entry in `stages()`; add after `_clone_sources`:

```python
    def _db_password(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]:
        """Persist the generated secret, or refuse to replace one the database already has.

        The spine resolved `ctx.secrets` before the stages ran — reading the
        file when it exists, minting `prefix + token_hex(8)` otherwise — so
        this stage's job is narrow: the evidence is the FILE (never recorded in
        the state file), and a missing file next to an existing `db-data`
        volume is a refusal. That volume was initialised with the password the
        file held; writing a new one would leave every later stage unable to
        log in, and the scripts' answer to that (wipe the volume silently) is
        exactly what the design rejects. The message names both things so the
        user can act on it.
        """
        plan = self.entry.install.password
        if plan.mode != "generated" or plan.file is None:
            yield "This server uses a fixed database password; nothing to write."
            return
        path = ctx.server_dir / plan.file
        if path.is_file():
            yield f"The database password is already in {plan.file}; keeping it."
            return
        volume = self._db_volume(ctx.server_dir)
        try:
            exists = self._seams.volume_exists(volume)
        except docker.DockerCommandError as exc:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"Docker would not say whether the database volume {volume} exists ({exc}), so "
                "this install cannot prove a new password is safe to write. Nothing was written."
            ) from exc
        if exists:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"{path} is gone, but this install's database volume {volume} still exists and "
                "was created with the password that file held. A new password would lock this "
                f"install out of its own database. Put {plan.file} back, or remove the old "
                "database (containers and volume) from the Server tab and install again."
            )
        try:
            _write_secret(path, ctx.secrets.db_password)
        except OSError as exc:
            raise InstallerError(f"{path} could not be written: {exc}") from exc
        yield f"Wrote a new database password to {plan.file} (readable only by you)."

    def _db_volume(self, server_dir: Path) -> str:
        """`<compose project>_<volume key>` — what `docker volume ls` shows for this install."""
        project = composegen.project_name(
            self.entry.id, server_dir, platform_id=self._seams.platform_id
        )
        return f"{project}_{DB_DATA_VOLUME}"
```

and at module level (after the class, before EOF):

```python
def _write_secret(path: Path, value: str) -> None:
    """Create the file 0600 from the first byte — never world-readable for even an instant.

    One trailing newline, the way `resolve_secrets()` expects to `.strip()` it back (A8).
    """
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as fh:
        fh.write(value + "\n")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — add `from yulon.catalog.families import dockerfile` to the imports; insert `Stage("write-dockerfile", self._write_dockerfile),` after the `db-password` entry; add the method after `_db_volume`:

```python
    def _write_dockerfile(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]:
        """Render `Dockerfile` + `.dockerignore` from the entry's template dir, marker rule applied.

        Marked and identical → nothing written (mtime untouched, so a later
        `compose build` sees no change); marked and different → rewritten;
        unmarked → refused by `dockerfile.write()`, because a Dockerfile this
        engine did not write is somebody's own build. `.git` is excluded by the
        template so the ~1 GB context is the tree, not its history (7.4a
        records the transfer time).
        """
        native_block = self._native()
        if native_block.dockerfile_dir is None:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"{self.entry.name} names no `dockerfile_dir`, so there is nothing to build from. "
                "That is a catalog error in the app, not something to fix on this machine."
            )
        template_dir = self.installers_root / native_block.dockerfile_dir
        try:
            text, ignore = dockerfile.render(template_dir, self._tokens(ctx))
            written = dockerfile.write(ctx.server_dir, text, ignore)
        except InstallerError:
            raise
        except (RuntimeError, OSError) as exc:
            # `ComposeGenError` (an unfilled token) and the module's own refusal
            # are RuntimeErrors; both become the sentence `run()` promises.
            raise InstallerError(str(exc)) from exc
        if not written:
            yield "The Dockerfile is already exactly what this install needs."
        for path in written:
            yield f"Wrote {path.name}"
```

```python
    def _extract(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]:
        """Pull dbc/maps/vmaps out of the user's client — mounted read-only, one `docker run` per tool.

        The Tortoise script's model (`-i /client -o /out`, `:ro`, `-u`) adopted
        for every CMaNGOS game: nothing is ever written into the client, no
        `sudo chown` afterwards, and an interrupted run leaves only our own
        partial folders under `data/`. Skipping is per TOOL and lives in
        `extract.run_plan()`: a completion record, the `produces` counts and
        the stage-level facts (plan hash, client path, required-file size and
        mtime) must all agree — which is why the required file and the client
        build are handed over here. This body only resolves what the module
        cannot know: which image, which uid, where the client is.
        """
        data = self._data()
        client_dir = ctx.client_dir
        if client_dir is None:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"{self.entry.name} needs the game client folder to extract its maps from, and "
                "none was given. Pick the client folder and try again."
            )
        data_dir = ctx.server_dir / DATA_DIR
        data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        image_ref = self._image_ref(ctx, data.extract.image)
        user_args = self._user_args()
        yield f"Extracting server data from {client_dir} into {data_dir} (the client is read-only)."
        yield from self._stream(
            lambda sink: extract.run_plan(
                data.extract,
                image_ref=image_ref,
                client_dir=client_dir,
                data_dir=data_dir,
                run_container=self._seams.run_container,
                user_args=user_args,
                sink=sink,
                cancel=ctx.cancel,
                required_file=data.client.required_file,
                client_build=self.entry.client.build,
            )
        )
        self._check_cancel(ctx.cancel)
        yield "Extraction finished."

    def _mmaps(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]:
        """Generate movement maps from the extracted maps; the long stage after the build.

        Same evidence rule as one extraction tool (record + count), and
        `required: false` (Tortoise) turns a shortfall into a warning inside
        `extract.run_mmaps()`, which also refuses when `data/` holds no
        extraction evidence at all. A resume after a kill wipes `data/mmaps`
        and starts over — that is the cancel note, and it is true because the
        generator has no resumable state of its own.
        """
        data = self._data()
        data_dir = ctx.server_dir / DATA_DIR
        data_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        image_ref = self._image_ref(ctx, data.extract.image)
        user_args = self._user_args()
        yield "Generating movement maps (this can take an hour or more)."
        yield from self._stream(
            lambda sink: extract.run_mmaps(
                data.mmaps,
                image_ref=image_ref,
                data_dir=data_dir,
                run_container=self._seams.run_container,
                user_args=user_args,
                sink=sink,
                cancel=ctx.cancel,
            )
        )
        self._check_cancel(ctx.cancel)
        yield "Map generation finished."
```

```python
    def _conf(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]:
        """Materialise the `.conf.dist` files once, then patch the table's keys in place.

        Copy-once and patch-in-place is the whole difference from the scripts,
        which overwrote every conf on every run: a user's other edits survive a
        resume. `conf.materialise()` copies only what is missing (create + cp +
        rm, no shell, host-owned files); `conf.apply_table()` rewrites
        `Key = value` lines through the same token grammar as everything else
        and reports which files changed — none means every key already read
        back equal, which is this stage's evidence. The password is in these
        files because the emulator reads files; they are 0600 for that reason.
        """
        data = self._data()
        etc_dir = ctx.server_dir / ETC_DIR
        image_ref = self._image_ref(ctx, data.extract.image)
        try:
            copied = conf.materialise(
                data.conf,
                image_ref=image_ref,
                etc_dir=etc_dir,
                copy_from_image=self._seams.copy_from_image,
            )
        except docker.DockerCommandError as exc:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"The configuration files could not be copied out of the server image: {exc}"
            ) from exc
        except OSError as exc:
            raise InstallerError(f"{etc_dir} could not be written: {exc}") from exc
        for path in copied:
            yield f"Copied {path.name} out of the server image."
        try:
            changed = conf.apply_table(data.conf, etc_dir, self._tokens(ctx))
        except InstallerError:
            raise
        except (RuntimeError, OSError) as exc:
            raise InstallerError(f"The configuration files could not be patched: {exc}") from exc
        if not changed:
            yield "The configuration files already say what this install needs."
        for path in changed:
            yield f"Patched {path.name}"
```

- Consumes: `StagedInstaller.stage_import(ctx, gate, service)` with `service=None` — runs the five-branch probe table and RETURNS after it (A7); `native.ImportGate`, `native.IMPORT_CANCEL_NOTE` (A10); `sqlplan.MarkerGate(plan, *, container, client, password, schemas, sql_query, exec_stdin)`, `sqlplan.expand(plan, server_dir, schemas, tokens)` (A10 — fills every `statement` through `composegen.fill`), `sqlplan.apply(runs, *, container, client, password, exec_stdin, sink, cancel)` (warn lines carry the server-dir-relative path, A10), `sqlplan.create_schemas(plan, *, container, client, password, schemas, user, charset, exec_stdin)`, `sqlplan.verify(plan, *, container, client, password, sql_query) -> tuple[str, ...]` (the failing rule names, A10), `sqlplan.write_marker(plan, *, container, client, password, exec_stdin)`, `sqlplan.MARKER_TABLE` (contract); `Seams.exec_stdin/sql_query` (K.2); `_tokens` (K.2); `SqlPlan.verify: tuple[VerifyRule, ...]`, `DbFacts`
- Produces: `_import(ctx)`, `_gate(ctx) -> native.ImportGate` (the seam the tests swap), `_schemas() -> dict[str, str]` — the identity mapping `{name: name for name in (db.auth, db.characters, db.world, *db.extra)}` (A10). The family has NO `_verify`: `sqlplan.verify()` is the one verify.

```python
# -- import -------------------------------------------------------------------

IMPORTED_OLD_PLAN = docker.ImportState(
    "imported",
    f"{sqlplan.MARKER_TABLE} holds plan 0000000000000000; this app's plan differs",
    complete=True,
)


def _server_with_sql(tmp_path: Path) -> Path:
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    server_dir.mkdir()
    lay = lay_sql(server_dir, SQL)
    for source in ENTRY.emulator.sources:
        dest = server_dir / source.dest
        dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        lay(dest)
    return server_dir


def test_schemas_carries_the_entrys_databases_and_nothing_the_entry_does_not_own() -> None:
    db = ENTRY.databases
    assert engine(Recorder())._schemas() == {
        name: name for name in (db.auth, db.characters, db.world, *db.extra)
    }


def test_the_import_applies_the_plans_dumps_in_the_plans_own_order(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = _server_with_sql(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.db_started = True
    rec.probe_answers = [ABSENT]
    said = list(engine(rec)._import(context(server_dir)))
    sql = rec.sql_calls
    assert any("CREATE DATABASE" in s for s in sql), "phase 0 creates the schemas"
    fed = [s for s in sql if s.startswith("-- ")]
    expected: list[str] = []
    for phase in SQL.phases:
        for pattern in list(phase.files) + list((phase.into_each or {}).values()):
            if "*" in pattern:
                expected += [f"-- {pattern.replace('*', f'{n:04d}')}" for n in (1, 2)]
            else:
                expected.append(f"-- {pattern}")
    assert fed == expected, "the plan's phases, in data order, files in natural order"
    assert "{{" not in "\n".join(sql), "every SQL statement was filled through composegen.fill"
    assert any("COUNT(*)" in s for s in sql), "every verify rule was asked"
    assert any(sqlplan.MARKER_TABLE in s for s in sql), "the marker was written last"
    marker_at = sql.index(next(s for s in sql if sqlplan.MARKER_TABLE in s))
    verify_at = sql.index(next(s for s in sql if "COUNT(*)" in s))
    assert marker_at > verify_at
    assert any("marked complete" in line for line in said)


def test_the_import_leaves_a_finished_one_alone_even_when_an_older_plan_wrote_it(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = _server_with_sql(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.db_started = True
    rec.probe_answers = [IMPORTED_OLD_PLAN]
    said = list(engine(rec)._import(context(server_dir)))
    assert "sql" not in rec.calls and "query" not in rec.calls
    assert any("leaving them alone" in line for line in said)


def test_the_import_clears_a_half_written_database_before_it_runs(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = _server_with_sql(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.db_started = True
    rec.probe_answers = [PARTIAL, IMPORTED]
    rec.reset_answer = (ENTRY.databases.world,)
    said = list(engine(rec)._import(context(server_dir)))
    assert rec.calls.index("reset") < rec.calls.index("sql")
    assert any(f"Cleared {ENTRY.databases.world}" in line for line in said)


def test_the_import_refuses_a_database_that_already_holds_somebodys_data(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = _server_with_sql(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.db_started = True
    rec.probe_answers = [POPULATED_HALF]
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="already hold data"):
        list(engine(rec)._import(context(server_dir)))
    assert "sql" not in rec.calls
    cold = Recorder()  # the database is not up: the real probe cannot answer
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="could not be asked"):
        list(engine(cold)._import(context(server_dir)))


def test_a_warn_phase_failure_names_the_file_by_its_server_relative_path_and_carries_on(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    server_dir = _server_with_sql(tmp_path)
    warn_phase = next(p for p in SQL.phases if p.on_error == "warn" and p.files)
    warn_file = warn_phase.files[0].replace("*", "0001")
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.db_started = True
    rec.probe_answers = [ABSENT]
    rec.failing_sql = f"-- {warn_file}"
    said = list(engine(rec)._import(context(server_dir)))
    assert any(warn_file in line for line in said), "a warn failure names the server-relative path"
    assert any(sqlplan.MARKER_TABLE in s for s in rec.sql_calls), "and the import still finishes"

    fail_phase = next(p for p in SQL.phases if p.on_error == "fail" and p.files)
    fail_file = fail_phase.files[0].replace("*", "0001")
    strict = Recorder()
    strict.db_started = True
    strict.probe_answers = [ABSENT]
    strict.failing_sql = f"-- {fail_file}"
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match=re.escape(fail_file.rsplit("/", 1)[-1])):
        list(engine(strict)._import(context(server_dir)))
    assert not any(sqlplan.MARKER_TABLE in s for s in strict.sql_calls), "no marker after a fail"


def test_a_verify_shortfall_refuses_and_writes_no_marker(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = _server_with_sql(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.db_started = True
    rec.probe_answers = [ABSENT]
    rec.query_answer = "3"
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError) as refusal:
        list(engine(rec)._import(context(server_dir)))
    rule = SQL.verify[0]
    assert rule.db in str(refusal.value) and str(rule.min) in str(refusal.value)
    assert not any(sqlplan.MARKER_TABLE in s for s in rec.sql_calls)


def test_the_import_cancel_note_is_said_at_the_import_and_nowhere_else() -> None:
    notes = {s.name: s.cancel_note for s in engine(Recorder()).stages()}
    assert notes["import"] == native.IMPORT_CANCEL_NOTE
    assert "half-written" in notes["import"]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — import `sqlplan` with the other stage kinds (`from yulon.catalog.families import conf, dockerfile, extract, sqlplan`), `from dataclasses import dataclass`, and extend the `native` import to `from yulon.catalog.native import (BUILD_CANCEL_NOTE, IMPORT_CANCEL_NOTE, INSTALL_REALM_HOST, ImportGate, Stage, StageContext, StagedInstaller)`; insert `Stage("import", self._import, cancel_note=IMPORT_CANCEL_NOTE),` after the `start-db` entry; add after `_conf`:

```python
    def _import(self, ctx: StageContext) -> Iterator[str]:
        """The spine decides (probe → skip/refuse/reset); this family imports when it says run.

        `stage_import()` with `service=None` runs the five-branch table over
        the `MarkerGate` and returns without importing (A7) — there is no
        compose one-shot here. What it decided is read off the gate's last
        answer rather than probed a second time: `imported` (any plan hash)
        or a complete `populated` means leave it alone; anything that got
        through the table means run.

        Then, in order: phase 0 (schemas, app user, grants — skipped when
        `create` is empty), every phase in data order with its statements
        filled through the one token mapping, `sqlplan.verify()`, and only
        then the marker. A plan whose verify fails writes NO marker, so a
        second press imports again instead of reading a hollow database as
        finished (the flaw two judges caught in the draft design).
        """
        plan = self._data().sql
        gate = _Remembering(self._gate(ctx))
        yield from self.stage_import(ctx, gate, None)
        seen = gate.last
        if seen is not None and (
            seen.state == "imported" or (seen.state == "populated" and seen.complete)
        ):
            return
        db = self._native().db
        container = self.entry.container_spec().db
        password = ctx.secrets.db_password
        schemas = self._schemas()
        try:
            if plan.create:
                yield f"Creating the databases ({', '.join(plan.create)}) and the {db.user} user."
                sqlplan.create_schemas(
                    plan,
                    container=container,
                    client=db.client,
                    password=password,
                    schemas=schemas,
                    user=db.user,
                    charset=db.charset,
                    exec_stdin=self._seams.exec_stdin,
                )
            runs = sqlplan.expand(plan, ctx.server_dir, schemas, self._tokens(ctx))
        except InstallerError:
            raise
        except (RuntimeError, OSError) as exc:
            raise InstallerError(f"The database import could not be prepared: {exc}") from exc
        yield f"Importing {len(runs)} SQL steps over {len(plan.phases)} phases. This takes a while."
        yield from self._stream(
            lambda sink: sqlplan.apply(
                runs,
                container=container,
                client=db.client,
                password=password,
                exec_stdin=self._seams.exec_stdin,
                sink=sink,
                cancel=ctx.cancel,
            )
        )
        self._check_cancel(ctx.cancel)
        try:
            failing = sqlplan.verify(
                plan,
                container=container,
                client=db.client,
                password=password,
                sql_query=self._seams.sql_query,
            )
        except InstallerError:
            raise
        except (docker.DockerCommandError, RuntimeError) as exc:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"The import finished but its databases could not be checked ({exc}). No "
                "completion marker was written, so the next install press checks again."
            ) from exc
        if failing:
            rules = "; ".join(
                f"{rule.db}: `{rule.query}` must answer at least {rule.min}" for rule in plan.verify
            )
            raise InstallerError(
                f"The import finished but these checks failed: {', '.join(failing)}. The rules "
                f"are {rules}. No completion marker was written, so the next install press "
                "imports again rather than starting a server with an empty world."
            )
        yield f"Checked {len(plan.verify)} database rule(s); every one holds."
        try:
            sqlplan.write_marker(
                plan,
                container=container,
                client=db.client,
                password=password,
                exec_stdin=self._seams.exec_stdin,
            )
        except (RuntimeError, OSError) as exc:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"The import finished but its completion marker could not be written: {exc}"
            ) from exc
        yield "The databases are imported and marked complete."

    def _gate(self, ctx: StageContext) -> ImportGate:
        """The family's `ImportGate`: the SQL plan's marker table, asked through the seams."""
        db = self._native().db
        return sqlplan.MarkerGate(
            self._data().sql,
            container=self.entry.container_spec().db,
            client=db.client,
            password=ctx.secrets.db_password,
            schemas=self._schemas(),
            sql_query=self._seams.sql_query,
            exec_stdin=self._seams.exec_stdin,
        )

    def _schemas(self) -> dict[str, str]:
        """The identity mapping over this game's schema names (A10).

        `sqlplan` looks a plan's `into`/`marker_db`/verify `db` up here, so a
        plan naming a schema the entry does not own is refused as "not one of
        this game's databases" before anything is written.
        """
        db = self.entry.databases
        return {name: name for name in (db.auth, db.characters, db.world, *db.extra)}


@dataclass
class _Remembering:
    """An `ImportGate` that remembers its last probe, so the family need not probe twice.

    The spine's `stage_import()` yields strings and returns nothing; what it
    decided is exactly what the gate last answered.
    """

    inner: ImportGate
    last: docker.ImportState | None = None

    def probe(self) -> docker.ImportState:
        self.last = self.inner.probe()
        return self.last

    def reset(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        return self.inner.reset()
```

```python
# -- the whole tuple through run() ---------------------------------------------

from yulon.catalog.families import FAMILIES, family_for  # noqa: E402 - grouped with its tests
from yulon.catalog.installer import installer_for  # noqa: E402

UNRECORDED = {"db-password", "start-db", "up", "ready"}


def test_every_cmangos_entry_dispatches_to_an_engine_that_runs_this_familys_stages() -> None:
    assert FAMILIES["cmangos"] is CmangosInstaller
    assert family_for(ENTRY) is CmangosInstaller
    assert isinstance(installer_for(ENTRY, platform_id=lambda: "linux"), CmangosInstaller)


def test_stage_names_is_the_pinned_tuple_now_that_every_stage_is_bound() -> None:
    eng = engine(Recorder())
    assert eng.stage_names() == CmangosInstaller.STAGE_NAMES
    assert {s.name for s in eng.stages() if not s.recorded} == UNRECORDED


def test_the_bound_stages_run_in_order_and_record_the_recorded_ones(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    client = client_folder(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    said = install(rec, server_dir, client)
    headers = [line[4:] for line in said if line.startswith("--- ")]
    assert headers == list(CmangosInstaller.STAGE_NAMES)
    assert [spec.dest for spec in rec.clones] == [server_dir / s.dest for s in ENTRY.emulator.sources]
    plan = ENTRY.install.password
    assert plan.file is not None
    minted = (server_dir / plan.file).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert minted.endswith("\n") and "\n" not in minted[:-1]
    assert re.fullmatch(re.escape(plan.prefix) + r"[0-9a-f]{16}", minted.strip()), minted
    assert (server_dir / "Dockerfile").is_file()
    assert (server_dir / composegen.BASE_FILE).is_file()
    assert "DB_ROOT_PASSWORD=" in (server_dir / composegen.DOTENV_FILE).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert CMANGOS is not None
    first_tool = f"run:{CMANGOS.extract.tools[0].argv[0]}"
    assert "build" in rec.calls and rec.calls.index("build") < rec.calls.index(first_tool)
    assert "start-db" in rec.calls and rec.calls.index("start-db") < rec.calls.index("sql")
    assert rec.calls[-1] == "start"
    recorded = native.read_state(server_dir, valid=CmangosInstaller.STAGE_NAMES)
    assert recorded is not None and recorded.family == "cmangos"
    assert recorded.completed == tuple(
        n for n in CmangosInstaller.STAGE_NAMES if n not in UNRECORDED
    )
    assert said[-1].endswith(f"is installed and running in {server_dir}")


def test_resume_after_a_failed_build_skips_what_finished_and_ends_running(tmp_path: Path) -> None:  # never written - see the note after this block
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    client = client_folder(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    rec.build_result = docker.AttachedRun(1, ("cc1plus: fatal error: Killed",))
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="the build failed"):
        install(rec, server_dir, client)
    state = native.read_state(server_dir, valid=CmangosInstaller.STAGE_NAMES)
    assert state is not None
    assert state.completed == ("clone-sources", "write-dockerfile", "generate-compose")
    assert "Killed" in state.last_error
    assert not rec.container_runs, "nothing after the build ran"

    rec.build_result = docker.AttachedRun(0, ("built",))
    rec.calls.clear()
    said = install(rec, server_dir, client)
    assert any(line.startswith("Already finished: clone-sources, write-dockerfile") for line in said)
    assert rec.calls.count("build") == 1
    assert rec.calls[-1] == "start"


def test_clone_sources_consults_the_record_under_its_own_stage_name(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    client = client_folder(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    install(rec, server_dir, client)
    runs, copies, sql = len(rec.container_runs), len(rec.copied), rec.calls.count("sql")
    rec.probe_answers = [IMPORTED]
    said = install(rec, server_dir, client)
    assert len(rec.container_runs) == runs, "extract + mmaps: records and counts in place"
    assert len(rec.copied) == copies, "conf: nothing re-copied"
    assert rec.calls.count("sql") == sql, "import: the marker says imported"
    assert any("already built" in line for line in said)
    assert said[-1].endswith(f"is installed and running in {server_dir}")


def test_db_password_refuses_when_the_file_is_gone_but_the_volume_exists(
    tmp_path: Path,
) -> None:
    server_dir = tmp_path / "srv"
    client = client_folder(tmp_path)
    rec = Recorder()
    install(rec, server_dir, client)
    (server_dir / ".db_password").unlink()
    rec.volumes.add(_db_volume(server_dir))
    rec.calls.clear()
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="db-data"):
        install(rec, server_dir, client)
    assert "build" not in rec.calls and not rec.container_runs
    state = native.read_state(server_dir, valid=CmangosInstaller.STAGE_NAMES)
    assert state is not None and "db-data" in state.last_error
```

Then `pyplan/style-guide.md` §3: insert these six rows directly after the `catalog/preflight.py` row (E.5 already added the 7.1 rows for `families/__init__.py`, `families/azerothcore.py`, `install_wiring.py` — A17; do not touch those):

```
| `catalog/families/cmangos.py` | The CMaNGOS-lineage stage tuple (`CmangosInstaller`): thin wrappers that pull each typed `install.native.cmangos` block off the entry and hand it to a stage-kind module; the `db-password` file + live-volume refusal; the one token mapping (`composegen.entry_tokens` + the install's own values) for Dockerfile, conf, SQL and verify | Contain a game literal (asserted over code via `ast`); run a subprocess itself; define a cancel note or a token the stage kinds already own; hold a template, a conf key, an argv or a SQL statement (those are `catalog.json`) |
| `catalog/families/clientdir.py` | Pure `validate(client_dir, spec)` → preflight `Check`s: refuse a missing folder, `Data/` or required file; warn on MPQ count, locale, the repack heuristic and free space | Prompt; check a build number it cannot verify; write into the client folder |
| `catalog/families/dockerfile.py` | Rendering `Dockerfile.tmpl` + `dockerignore.tmpl` with the generated-file marker; refusing to overwrite an unmarked file | Run a build; know which game it renders for |
| `catalog/families/extract.py` | One `docker run --rm` per extraction tool — client `:ro` at `/client`, `data/` rw at `/out`, `--user uid:gid` on Linux; the per-tool completion records + count evidence in `data/.yulon-extract.json`; the mmaps run; `EXTRACT_CANCEL_NOTE`/`MMAPS_CANCEL_NOTE` | Write into the client folder; `sudo chown`; skip a tool on the file count alone; generate mmaps with no extraction evidence |
| `catalog/families/conf.py` | Pure `patch()` over ini `Key = value` lines (`match_commented` for `# Key =`); `materialise()` copying `.conf.dist` out of the image once via create/cp/rm; `apply_table()` reporting what changed | Re-copy over a file the user has edited; put the password anywhere but the 0600 conf files |
| `catalog/families/sqlplan.py` | `expand()` (relative globs, natural sort == `ls -v`, gzip, statements filled through `composegen.fill`), `apply()` (ordered phases over `docker exec -i`, per-phase `fail`/`warn` naming the server-relative path), `create_schemas()`, `verify()`, the completion marker and `MarkerGate` (probe + reset) | Hide an error; drop a schema that holds player data; put the password in argv or SQL text |
```

## Group I — client folder, Dockerfile, extraction, and the client facts in preflight (plan 7.3)

Scope notes for the executor (not tasks): every module below lives under `yulon/catalog/families/`, which the 7.1 tasks create (`families/__init__.py` with `FAMILIES`/`family_for`). The catalog models these tasks consume (`ClientSpec`, `MpqDepth`, `ExtractTool`, `ExtractPlan`, `RetrySpec`, `MmapPlan`) and the docker primitives (`ContainerRun`, `Mount`, `run_container`) are contract.md 7.3 types built by the catalog/docker group; the tests here construct the pydantic models directly and never touch `catalog.json`. Every template, Dockerfile, conf value and SQL statement in this phase is filled through the one public `composegen.fill(text, tokens)` (amendment A6); `CORE_DIR` is the in-image install prefix (`/opt/mangos`), never a source checkout path. No line numbers are cited (amendment A18): where a task points into an existing file it says "grep for `<symbol>`", and the executor re-derives the position with `git grep -n` at execution time. The cancel notes `extract.EXTRACT_CANCEL_NOTE` and `extract.MMAPS_CANCEL_NOTE` are defined here once and imported by `families/cmangos.py` (A10), never redefined.

```python
"""Tests for `yulon.catalog.families.clientdir` — every rule, refuse vs warn, for the three specs.

Pure: a `tmp_path` tree stands in for the client and `free_bytes` is a lambda. A refusal is
what the design asks for where the scripts asked "Continue anyway?" — the engine cannot ask.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from yulon.catalog.catalog import ClientSpec
from yulon.catalog.families import clientdir
from yulon.catalog.preflight import GIB

PLENTY = 100 * GIB

TBC = ClientSpec(
    required_file="Data/expansion.MPQ",
    min_mpq=6,
    mpq_depth="recursive",
    locale_mpq_required=True,
    near_client_warn_gb=8,
)
VANILLA = ClientSpec(required_file="Data/dbc.MPQ", min_mpq=5, mpq_depth=1)
TORTOISE = ClientSpec(required_file=None, min_mpq=5, mpq_depth=2)


def client(root: Path, *, mpqs: int = 8, locale: str | None = "enUS", required: bool = True) -> Path:
    """A client folder that passes TBC's spec unless told otherwise."""
    data = root / "client" / "Data"
    data.mkdir(parents=True)
    for index in range(mpqs):
        (data / f"patch-{index}.MPQ").write_bytes(b"MPQ")
    if required:
        (data / "expansion.MPQ").write_bytes(b"MPQ")
        (data / "dbc.MPQ").write_bytes(b"MPQ")
    if locale is not None:
        (data / locale).mkdir()
        (data / locale / f"locale-{locale}.MPQ").write_bytes(b"MPQ")
    return root / "client"


def verdicts(checks: tuple[object, ...]) -> dict[str, str]:
    return {check.name: check.verdict for check in checks}  # type: ignore[attr-defined]


def test_no_client_folder_is_a_refusal_that_names_what_to_pick() -> None:
    (check,) = clientdir.validate(None, TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert check.verdict == "refuse"
    assert "Data" in check.remedy


def test_a_folder_without_data_is_refused(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "client").mkdir()
    (check,) = clientdir.validate(tmp_path / "client", TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert check.verdict == "refuse"
    assert "Data" in check.detail


def test_a_missing_required_file_is_refused_by_name(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path, required=False)
    checks = clientdir.validate(folder, TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    refused = [check for check in checks if check.verdict == "refuse"]
    assert len(refused) == 1
    assert "expansion.MPQ" in refused[0].detail


def test_required_file_none_disables_only_that_rule(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path, required=False)
    checks = clientdir.validate(folder, TORTOISE, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert not [check for check in checks if check.verdict == "refuse"]


def test_a_good_client_passes_every_rule(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path)
    checks = clientdir.validate(folder, TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert set(verdicts(checks).values()) == {"pass"}, verdicts(checks)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — create `yulon/catalog/families/clientdir.py`:

```python
"""The client folder: refuse what cannot extract, warn about what usually does not.

`validate()` is pure and returns preflight `Check`s, not questions. The scripts
asked "Continue anyway? (server WILL fail to load maps)" at every doubt; the
engine cannot ask, so each doubt is either a refusal (the folder, `Data/` or
the expansion's own MPQ is missing — nothing can be extracted from that) or a
warning (too few MPQs, no locale folder, the repack smell, a nearly full
drive — extraction usually still works, and the count gate in `extract.py` is
the real check). The build number is reported by nothing here: no script read
it either, and a number the folder cannot prove is not a fact.

Per-game data (`ClientSpec`) is the only thing that differs between the three
CMaNGOS clients, so this module holds no game literal — style-guide §3.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from pathlib import Path

from yulon.catalog.catalog import ClientSpec, MpqDepth
from yulon.catalog.preflight import GIB, Check
from yulon.log import get_logger

logger = get_logger(__name__)

DATA_DIR = "Data"
"""Where every WoW client keeps its archives; its absence means "not a client"."""

CLIENT_CHECK = "the client folder"
REQUIRED_CHECK = "the client's expansion data"
MPQ_CHECK = "the client's archives"
LOCALE_CHECK = "the client's locale"
REPACK_CHECK = "the client's origin"
SPACE_CHECK = "free space next to the client"

FreeBytes = Callable[[Path], int | None]


def validate(client_dir: Path | None, spec: ClientSpec, *, free_bytes: FreeBytes) -> tuple[Check, ...]:
    """Every rule for one client folder, refusals first, in the order a user reads them.

    Returns as soon as a refusal makes the remaining rules meaningless (no
    folder, no `Data/`): warning about the MPQ count of a folder that is not a
    client would bury the one line that matters.

    `free_bytes` is a seam and not `shutil.disk_usage` so the whole table is
    testable on a `tmp_path`; preflight passes its own `_free_bytes`.
    """
    if client_dir is None:
        return (
            Check(
                CLIENT_CHECK,
                "refuse",
                "no client folder was chosen, and this server's maps are extracted from one",
                f"Pick the game folder that holds the {DATA_DIR} directory, then try again.",
            ),
        )
    if not client_dir.is_dir():
        return (
            Check(
                CLIENT_CHECK,
                "refuse",
                f"{client_dir} is not a folder",
                f"Pick the game folder that holds the {DATA_DIR} directory, then try again.",
            ),
        )
    data = client_dir / DATA_DIR
    if not data.is_dir():
        return (
            Check(
                CLIENT_CHECK,
                "refuse",
                f"{client_dir} has no {DATA_DIR} directory, so it is not a game client",
                "Pick the folder the game itself is installed in, not a launcher or a backup.",
            ),
        )
    checks: list[Check] = [Check(CLIENT_CHECK, "pass", f"{client_dir} has a {DATA_DIR} directory")]
    if spec.required_file is not None:
        required = client_dir.joinpath(*spec.required_file.split("/"))
        if not required.is_file():
            checks.append(
                Check(
                    REQUIRED_CHECK,
                    "refuse",
                    f"{spec.required_file} is missing from {client_dir}, so this is not the "
                    "expansion this server needs, or the client is incomplete",
                    "Point the install at a complete client of the right expansion.",
                )
            )
            return tuple(checks)
        checks.append(Check(REQUIRED_CHECK, "pass", f"{spec.required_file} is there"))
    checks.extend(_warnings(client_dir, data, spec, free_bytes))
    return tuple(checks)


def _warnings(client_dir: Path, data: Path, spec: ClientSpec, free_bytes: FreeBytes) -> list[Check]:
    """The rules that warn (Task I.2 fills this in); nothing yet."""
    return []


def mpq_files(data: Path, depth: MpqDepth) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
    """Every `*.MPQ` (any case) under `data`, to `depth` levels or everywhere.

    Depth 1 is `Data/` itself, depth 2 adds `Data/<locale>/` — the shapes the
    three clients have, spelled as data so the module has no game in it.
    Raises OSError where the folder cannot be read; the caller decides what an
    unreadable client means.
    """
    found = data.rglob("*") if depth == "recursive" else _to_depth(data, depth)
    return tuple(sorted(path for path in found if path.is_file() and path.suffix.lower() == ".mpq"))


def _to_depth(root: Path, depth: int) -> Iterator[Path]:
    level = [root]
    for _ in range(depth):
        deeper: list[Path] = []
        for folder in level:
            for entry in folder.iterdir():
                yield entry
                if entry.is_dir():
                    deeper.append(entry)
        level = deeper


def locale_dirs(data: Path) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
    """Subfolders of `Data/` holding an MPQ — `enUS`, `deDE`; what a repack strips."""
    return tuple(
        sorted(
            folder
            for folder in data.iterdir()
            if folder.is_dir()
            and any(f.is_file() and f.suffix.lower() == ".mpq" for f in folder.iterdir())
        )
    )
```

```python
def test_too_few_mpqs_at_the_spec_depth_warns_and_does_not_refuse(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path, mpqs=1, locale=None)
    checks = clientdir.validate(folder, VANILLA, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert verdicts(checks)["the client's archives"] == "warn"
    assert not [check for check in checks if check.verdict == "refuse"]


def test_mpq_depth_one_is_the_data_folder_itself(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path, mpqs=2, locale="enUS")
    data = folder / "Data"
    assert len(clientdir.mpq_files(data, 1)) == 4  # 2 patches + expansion + dbc
    assert len(clientdir.mpq_files(data, 2)) == 5
    assert len(clientdir.mpq_files(data, "recursive")) == 5
    (data / "enUS" / "deep").mkdir()
    (data / "enUS" / "deep" / "x.mpq").write_bytes(b"MPQ")
    assert len(clientdir.mpq_files(data, 2)) == 5
    assert len(clientdir.mpq_files(data, "recursive")) == 6


def test_a_missing_locale_folder_warns_only_when_the_spec_requires_one(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path, locale=None)
    tbc = verdicts(clientdir.validate(folder, TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY))
    assert tbc["the client's locale"] == "warn"
    vanilla = verdicts(clientdir.validate(folder, VANILLA, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY))
    assert "the client's locale" not in vanilla


def test_realmlist_at_the_root_with_no_locale_smells_like_a_repack(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path, locale=None)
    (folder / "realmlist.wtf").write_text("set realmlist 127.0.0.1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    checks = clientdir.validate(folder, VANILLA, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert verdicts(checks)["the client's origin"] == "warn"
    with_locale = client(tmp_path / "other", locale="enUS")
    (with_locale / "realmlist.wtf").write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")
    checks = clientdir.validate(with_locale, VANILLA, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert verdicts(checks)["the client's origin"] == "pass"


def test_little_free_space_next_to_the_client_warns_with_both_numbers(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path)
    low = verdicts(clientdir.validate(folder, TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: 3 * GIB))
    assert low["free space next to the client"] == "warn"
    unknown = verdicts(clientdir.validate(folder, TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: None))
    assert unknown["free space next to the client"] == "unchecked"


def test_an_unreadable_data_folder_is_unchecked_not_too_few_archives(
    tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path)

    def boom(_data: Path, _depth: object) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
        raise OSError("permission denied")

    monkeypatch.setattr(clientdir, "mpq_files", boom)
    checks = clientdir.validate(folder, TBC, free_bytes=lambda _p: PLENTY)
    assert verdicts(checks)["the client's archives"] == "unchecked"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — replace the I.1 stub `_warnings` in `yulon/catalog/families/clientdir.py` with:

```python
REPACK_FILE = "realmlist.wtf"
"""A pre-configured client ships one at the root; a retail install keeps it under Data/<locale>/."""


def _warnings(client_dir: Path, data: Path, spec: ClientSpec, free_bytes: FreeBytes) -> list[Check]:
    """The rules that warn. Extraction usually survives each, so none refuses."""
    checks = [_mpq_check(data, spec)]
    if spec.locale_mpq_required:
        checks.append(_locale_check(data))
    checks.append(_repack_check(client_dir, data))
    checks.append(_space_check(client_dir, spec, free_bytes))
    return checks


def _mpq_check(data: Path, spec: ClientSpec) -> Check:
    """Fewer archives than the expansion ships is the incomplete-download smell.

    A warning, not a refusal: a client with the right expansion MPQ but fewer
    patches extracts, and the `produces` counts in `extract.py` are what
    prove it did.
    """
    try:
        found = mpq_files(data, spec.mpq_depth)
    except OSError as exc:
        logger.info(f"could not list the archives under {data}: {exc}")
        return Check(
            MPQ_CHECK,
            "unchecked",
            f"the archives under {data} could not be listed — that is not a pass",
            "Check the folder's permissions if extraction fails.",
        )
    where = "anywhere under" if spec.mpq_depth == "recursive" else f"{spec.mpq_depth} deep in"
    if len(found) < spec.min_mpq:
        return Check(
            MPQ_CHECK,
            "warn",
            f"{len(found)} MPQ archives {where} {data}; a complete client has at least "
            f"{spec.min_mpq}",
            "If extraction reports too few maps, the client download is incomplete.",
        )
    return Check(MPQ_CHECK, "pass", f"{len(found)} MPQ archives {where} {data}")


def _locale_check(data: Path) -> Check:
    try:
        locales = locale_dirs(data)
    except OSError as exc:
        logger.info(f"could not look for locale folders under {data}: {exc}")
        return Check(LOCALE_CHECK, "unchecked", f"{data} could not be read — that is not a pass")
    if not locales:
        return Check(
            LOCALE_CHECK,
            "warn",
            f"no locale folder with archives under {data} (enUS, deDE, ...)",
            "This expansion's DBC extraction reads the locale archives; expect a shortfall.",
        )
    names = ", ".join(folder.name for folder in locales)
    return Check(LOCALE_CHECK, "pass", f"locale archives in {names}")


def _repack_check(client_dir: Path, data: Path) -> Check:
    """`realmlist.wtf` at the root and no locale folder: a stripped, pre-pointed repack.

    Such clients have had their archives rearranged and extract incompletely
    or not at all. The heuristic is exactly what the scripts eyeballed; it
    warns because a hand-moved realmlist on a full client is also possible.
    """
    try:
        stripped = (client_dir / REPACK_FILE).is_file() and not locale_dirs(data)
    except OSError as exc:
        logger.info(f"could not apply the repack heuristic to {client_dir}: {exc}")
        return Check(REPACK_CHECK, "unchecked", f"{client_dir} could not be read")
    if stripped:
        return Check(
            REPACK_CHECK,
            "warn",
            f"{REPACK_FILE} sits at the root of {client_dir} and there is no locale folder, "
            "which is how repacks look",
            "A repack often extracts incompletely; a clean client of this expansion is safer.",
        )
    return Check(REPACK_CHECK, "pass", "nothing suggests a repack")


def _space_check(client_dir: Path, spec: ClientSpec, free_bytes: FreeBytes) -> Check:
    """Room on the client's own drive, because extraction reads it through Docker's share.

    On Docker Desktop a shared folder is cached on the drive it lives on, and
    the scripts' extractors were seen to stall on a drive that had no room
    for that cache. Data carries the figure; nothing is measured here.
    """
    free = free_bytes(client_dir)
    if free is None:
        return Check(
            SPACE_CHECK,
            "unchecked",
            f"the free space on the drive holding {client_dir} could not be measured",
            f"Make sure it has at least {spec.near_client_warn_gb:.0f} GB free.",
        )
    gigabytes = free / GIB
    if gigabytes < spec.near_client_warn_gb:
        return Check(
            SPACE_CHECK,
            "warn",
            f"{gigabytes:.0f} GB free on the drive holding the client; "
            f"{spec.near_client_warn_gb:.0f} GB is the comfortable figure",
            "Extraction reads the client through Docker's file sharing, which caches on that drive.",
        )
    return Check(SPACE_CHECK, "pass", f"{gigabytes:.0f} GB free on the drive holding the client")
```

### Task I.3: dockerfile — render with the generated marker, write under the marker rule

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Produces: `dockerfile.render(template_dir: Path, tokens: Mapping[str, str]) -> tuple[str, str]`; `dockerfile.write(server_dir: Path, dockerfile: str, dockerignore: str) -> tuple[Path, ...]`; `dockerfile.DockerfileError`; `dockerfile.DOCKERFILE = "Dockerfile"`, `dockerfile.DOCKERIGNORE = ".dockerignore"`, `dockerfile.TEMPLATE = "Dockerfile.tmpl"`, `dockerfile.IGNORE_TEMPLATE = "dockerignore.tmpl"` (the `write-dockerfile` stage in cmangos.py calls render then write)

```python
"""Tests for `yulon.catalog.families.dockerfile`: the marker on every render, refusal of anything unmarked."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from yulon.catalog import composegen
from yulon.catalog.families import dockerfile

TOKENS = {"CORE_DIR": "/opt/mangos", "MAKE_JOBS": "2", "CLIENT_BUILD": "8606"}


def templates(root: Path) -> Path:
    folder = root / "native"
    folder.mkdir()
    (folder / "Dockerfile.tmpl").write_text(
        "FROM debian:12\nRUN cmake -DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX={{CORE_DIR}} /src\nRUN make -j{{MAKE_JOBS}}\n",
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
    (folder / "dockerignore.tmpl").write_text(".git\n**/.git\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return folder


def test_render_fills_tokens_and_starts_both_files_with_the_marker(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    text, ignore = dockerfile.render(templates(tmp_path), TOKENS)
    assert text.startswith(composegen.GENERATED_MARKER + "\n")
    assert ignore.startswith(composegen.GENERATED_MARKER + "\n")
    assert "-DCMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX=/opt/mangos /src" in text and "make -j2" in text
    assert "{{" not in text and "{{" not in ignore
    assert ".git\n" in ignore


def test_render_refuses_an_unknown_token(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = templates(tmp_path)
    (folder / "Dockerfile.tmpl").write_text("FROM x\nRUN {{NOT_A_TOKEN}}\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(dockerfile.DockerfileError, match="NOT_A_TOKEN"):
        dockerfile.render(folder, TOKENS)


def test_render_names_a_missing_template(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(dockerfile.DockerfileError, match="Dockerfile.tmpl"):
        dockerfile.render(tmp_path, TOKENS)


def test_write_lays_both_files_and_a_second_identical_write_touches_nothing(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    text, ignore = dockerfile.render(templates(tmp_path), TOKENS)
    server = tmp_path / "server"
    server.mkdir()
    written = dockerfile.write(server, text, ignore)
    assert written == (server / "Dockerfile", server / ".dockerignore")
    assert (server / "Dockerfile").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == text
    assert dockerfile.write(server, text, ignore) == ()


def test_write_rewrites_a_marked_file_whose_text_changed(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    text, ignore = dockerfile.render(templates(tmp_path), TOKENS)
    server = tmp_path / "server"
    server.mkdir()
    dockerfile.write(server, text, ignore)
    newer, _ = dockerfile.render(templates(tmp_path / "v2"), {**TOKENS, "MAKE_JOBS": "4"})
    assert dockerfile.write(server, newer, ignore) == (server / "Dockerfile",)
    assert "make -j4" in (server / "Dockerfile").read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def test_write_refuses_a_dockerfile_it_did_not_write(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    text, ignore = dockerfile.render(templates(tmp_path), TOKENS)
    server = tmp_path / "server"
    server.mkdir()
    (server / "Dockerfile").write_text("FROM somebody-elses:image\n", encoding="utf-8")
    with pytest.raises(dockerfile.DockerfileError, match="not written by Yu'lon"):
        dockerfile.write(server, text, ignore)
    assert (server / "Dockerfile").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "FROM somebody-elses:image\n"
    assert not (server / ".dockerignore").exists()


def test_write_refuses_text_that_carries_no_marker(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    server = tmp_path / "server"
    server.mkdir()
    with pytest.raises(dockerfile.DockerfileError, match="marker"):
        dockerfile.write(server, "FROM x\n", f"{composegen.GENERATED_MARKER}\n.git\n")
    assert not (server / "Dockerfile").exists()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — create `yulon/catalog/families/dockerfile.py`:

```python
"""Render a game's `Dockerfile` and `.dockerignore` from templates, under the marker rule.

The rule is `composegen.GENERATED_MARKER`'s, reused rather than restated: a
file that begins with the marker is ours to rewrite, a file that does not is
refused untouched. The incident behind it (`rust-prior-art.md` §1) was a
generator overwriting a real server's compose file; a Dockerfile is the same
shape of file in the same folder, so it gets the same rule and the same
first-line test (`composegen.is_ours`).

This module renders and writes. It never runs a build — the spine's `build`
stage does — and it holds no template text: that is
`catalog/installers/<game>/native/{Dockerfile.tmpl,dockerignore.tmpl}`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path

from yulon.catalog import composegen
from yulon.log import get_logger

logger = get_logger(__name__)

DOCKERFILE = "Dockerfile"
DOCKERIGNORE = ".dockerignore"
TEMPLATE = "Dockerfile.tmpl"
IGNORE_TEMPLATE = "dockerignore.tmpl"


class DockerfileError(RuntimeError):
    """A template could not be rendered, or a file in the way is not ours."""


def render(template_dir: Path, tokens: Mapping[str, str]) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """(Dockerfile text, .dockerignore text), each beginning with the marker as a `#` comment.

    One `fill()` for every template in the app — `composegen.fill`, which
    refuses an unfilled `{{TOKEN}}` — so a Dockerfile cannot ship a literal
    placeholder that `docker build` would happily read as a path.
    """
    return (
        _render_one(template_dir / TEMPLATE, tokens),
        _render_one(template_dir / IGNORE_TEMPLATE, tokens),
    )


def _render_one(path: Path, tokens: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
    try:
        template = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except OSError as exc:
        raise DockerfileError(f"the template {path} could not be read: {exc}") from exc
    try:
        filled = composegen.fill(template, tokens)
    except composegen.ComposeGenError as exc:
        raise DockerfileError(f"{path}: {exc}") from exc
    return f"{composegen.GENERATED_MARKER}\n{filled}"


def write(server_dir: Path, dockerfile: str, dockerignore: str) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
    """Lay both files in `server_dir`; the paths actually written come back.

    Unchanged text is left alone so the file's mtime does not move and a
    resume's evidence ("marker + text equal") stays cheap. Both the text
    handed in and the file already on disk must carry the marker: the first
    guards against a caller that skipped `render()`, the second is the rule.
    Nothing is written until both files have been checked, so a refusal
    leaves the folder exactly as it was.

    Raises:
        DockerfileError: an unmarked file is in the way, or the text has no marker.
    """
    planned = ((DOCKERFILE, dockerfile), (DOCKERIGNORE, dockerignore))
    for name, text in planned:
        if not text.startswith(composegen.GENERATED_MARKER):
            raise DockerfileError(f"the {name} text carries no generated-file marker; refusing to write it")
        path = server_dir / name
        if not composegen.is_ours(path):
            raise DockerfileError(
                f"{path} was not written by Yu'lon, so it was not touched and nothing was "
                "installed. Point the install at an empty folder, or move that file aside."
            )
    written: list[Path] = []
    for name, text in planned:
        path = server_dir / name
        if path.exists() and path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == text:
            continue
        path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8", newline="\n")
        logger.info(f"wrote {path}")
        written.append(path)
    return tuple(written)
```

### Task I.4: extract — the evidence file and the three-part `tool_satisfied` rule

**Files:**
- Create: `yulon/catalog/families/extract.py`
- Test: `tests/test_extract.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `catalog.ExtractTool`, `catalog.ExtractPlan` (contract 7.3; pydantic `_Strict`, so `plan.model_dump(mode="json")` is available)
- Produces: `extract.EVIDENCE_FILE = ".yulon-extract.json"`; `ToolRecord(name, argv_hash, finished_unix)`; `Evidence(plan_hash, client_path, required_file_size, required_file_mtime, tools)`; `read_evidence(data_dir) -> Evidence | None`; `write_evidence(data_dir, evidence) -> None`; `tool_satisfied(tool, data_dir, evidence, expected) -> bool`; helpers later tasks use: `argv_hash(argv: Sequence[str]) -> str`, `plan_hash(plan: ExtractPlan) -> str`, `expected_evidence(plan, client_dir, required_file: str | None) -> Evidence`, `shortfall(produces: Mapping[str, int], data_dir) -> dict[str, tuple[int, int]]`, `with_record(evidence, record) -> Evidence`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

```python
"""Tests for `yulon.catalog.families.extract`.

The rule under test is three-part and every part is a real incident shape: a
tool killed after it passed its count threshold (record missing → re-run), a
resume pointed at another client (stage facts differ → everything re-runs),
and an edited argv (hash differs → that tool re-runs).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import threading
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from yulon import docker
from yulon.catalog.catalog import ExtractPlan, ExtractTool, MmapPlan, RetrySpec
from yulon.catalog.families import extract
from yulon.catalog.installer import InstallerError

AD = ExtractTool(name="dbc and maps", argv=("/opt/bin/ad", "-i", "/client", "-o", "/out"), produces={"dbc": 3, "maps": 2})
VMAP = ExtractTool(name="vmap extract", argv=("/opt/bin/vmap_extractor", "-d", "/client/Data"), produces={"Buildings": 2})
ASSEMBLE = ExtractTool(name="vmap assemble", argv=("/opt/bin/vmap_assembler", "Buildings", "vmaps"), produces={"vmaps": 2})
PLAN = ExtractPlan(image="server", tools=(AD, VMAP, ASSEMBLE))
REQUIRED = "Data/expansion.MPQ"


def client(root: Path) -> Path:
    folder = root / "client"
    (folder / "Data").mkdir(parents=True)
    (folder / "Data" / "expansion.MPQ").write_bytes(b"MPQ" * 100)
    return folder


def fill(data_dir: Path, folder: str, count: int) -> None:
    (data_dir / folder).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for index in range(count):
        (data_dir / folder / f"f{index}").write_bytes(b"x")


def test_evidence_round_trips_and_a_missing_or_broken_file_reads_as_none(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    assert extract.read_evidence(tmp_path) is None
    evidence = extract.Evidence("abc", "/c", 300, 1700000000, (extract.ToolRecord("ad", "h", 1),))
    extract.write_evidence(tmp_path, evidence)
    assert (tmp_path / extract.EVIDENCE_FILE).is_file()
    assert extract.read_evidence(tmp_path) == evidence
    (tmp_path / extract.EVIDENCE_FILE).write_text("{not json", encoding="utf-8")
    assert extract.read_evidence(tmp_path) is None


def test_expected_evidence_reads_the_required_file_facts_and_none_without_one(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path)
    expected = extract.expected_evidence(PLAN, folder, REQUIRED)
    assert expected.client_path == str(folder.resolve())
    assert expected.required_file_size == 300
    assert expected.required_file_mtime == int((folder / "Data" / "expansion.MPQ").stat().st_mtime)
    assert expected.plan_hash == extract.plan_hash(PLAN)
    bare = extract.expected_evidence(PLAN, folder, None)
    assert bare.required_file_size is None and bare.required_file_mtime is None


def test_plan_hash_is_stable_and_changes_with_the_plan() -> None:
    assert extract.plan_hash(PLAN) == extract.plan_hash(PLAN)
    assert len(extract.plan_hash(PLAN)) == 16
    other = ExtractPlan(image="server", tools=(AD, VMAP, ASSEMBLE), ulimit_stack_unlimited=True)
    assert extract.plan_hash(other) != extract.plan_hash(PLAN)


def test_a_tool_with_passing_counts_but_no_record_is_not_satisfied(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path)
    data = tmp_path / "data"
    fill(data, "dbc", 3)
    fill(data, "maps", 2)
    expected = extract.expected_evidence(PLAN, folder, REQUIRED)
    assert extract.tool_satisfied(AD, data, None, expected) is False
    assert extract.tool_satisfied(AD, data, expected, expected) is False  # no ToolRecord yet
    recorded = extract.with_record(expected, extract.ToolRecord(AD.name, extract.argv_hash(AD.argv), 5))
    assert extract.tool_satisfied(AD, data, recorded, expected) is True


def test_a_record_with_passing_counts_but_other_stage_facts_is_not_satisfied(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path)
    data = tmp_path / "data"
    fill(data, "dbc", 3)
    fill(data, "maps", 2)
    expected = extract.expected_evidence(PLAN, folder, REQUIRED)
    record = extract.ToolRecord(AD.name, extract.argv_hash(AD.argv), 5)
    other_client = extract.with_record(
        extract.Evidence(expected.plan_hash, "/somewhere/else", 300, expected.required_file_mtime, ()), record
    )
    assert extract.tool_satisfied(AD, data, other_client, expected) is False
    other_argv = extract.with_record(expected, extract.ToolRecord(AD.name, "0000000000000000", 5))
    assert extract.tool_satisfied(AD, data, other_argv, expected) is False


def test_a_record_whose_counts_fell_short_is_not_satisfied(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    folder = client(tmp_path)
    data = tmp_path / "data"
    fill(data, "dbc", 3)
    fill(data, "maps", 1)
    expected = extract.expected_evidence(PLAN, folder, REQUIRED)
    recorded = extract.with_record(expected, extract.ToolRecord(AD.name, extract.argv_hash(AD.argv), 5))
    assert extract.tool_satisfied(AD, data, recorded, expected) is False
    assert extract.shortfall(AD.produces, data) == {"maps": (1, 2)}


def test_with_record_replaces_a_record_of_the_same_name() -> None:
    evidence = extract.Evidence("p", "/c", None, None, (extract.ToolRecord("a", "1", 1),))
    again = extract.with_record(evidence, extract.ToolRecord("a", "2", 2))
    assert again.tools == (extract.ToolRecord("a", "2", 2),)
    assert extract.with_record(again, extract.ToolRecord("b", "3", 3)).tools[1].name == "b"
```
