- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — in `yulon/catalog/families/sqlplan.py` add `import time` to the imports and append after `_check_cancel`:

```python
def create_schemas(
    plan: SqlPlan,
    *,
    container: str,
    client: str,
    password: str,
    schemas: Mapping[str, str],
    user: str,
    charset: str,
    exec_stdin: ExecStdin,
) -> None:
    """The implicit phase 0: databases, the app user, its grants. Skipped when `create` is empty.

    Idempotent (`IF NOT EXISTS`, then `ALTER USER` to the current secret) so a
    resume over a database that already has them is a no-op rather than an
    error. One secret: the app user gets the same password the root account
    has, exactly as the scripts did with one `DB_PASSWORD` — the emulator
    connects as `user`, the import streams as root. The password appears in
    this SQL text, unavoidably (`IDENTIFIED BY`), and only here: it goes over
    stdin, and this text is never logged or yielded.
    """
    if not plan.create:
        return
    _refuse_quotes(password, "the database password")
    _refuse_quotes(user, "the database user name")
    names = [_schema_named(name, schemas) for name in plan.create]
    lines = [f"CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS `{name}` CHARACTER SET {charset};" for name in names]
    lines.append(f"CREATE USER IF NOT EXISTS '{user}'@'%' IDENTIFIED BY '{password}';")
    lines.append(f"ALTER USER '{user}'@'%' IDENTIFIED BY '{password}';")
    lines += [f"GRANT ALL PRIVILEGES ON `{name}`.* TO '{user}'@'%';" for name in names]
    lines.append("FLUSH PRIVILEGES;")
    _run_sql(
        "\n".join(lines) + "\n",
        what="creating the databases and the application user",
        container=container,
        client=client,
        password=password,
        schema=None,
        exec_stdin=exec_stdin,
    )


def verify(
    plan: SqlPlan,
    *,
    container: str,
    client: str,
    password: str,
    sql_query: SqlQuery,
) -> tuple[str, ...]:
    """Every verify rule, in order; one sentence per rule that FAILED, `()` when all pass.

    The gate before `write_marker()`: the family raises when this is non-empty
    and writes no marker, because an import whose `warn` phases all failed
    would otherwise read `imported` forever. A rule that cannot be answered
    (the client fails, or answers something that is not a count) is a failing
    rule too, never an exception — one code path for the caller, and the
    sentence says which it was. `rule.db` is the schema as it is on the
    server: the plan's names are the server's names (A10).
    """
    failed: list[str] = []
    for rule in plan.verify:
        try:
            answer = sql_query(container, client, password, rule.db, rule.query).strip()
        except docker.DockerCommandError as exc:
            failed.append(f"{rule.db}: `{rule.query}` could not be answered ({exc})")
            continue
        try:
            count = int(answer.splitlines()[0]) if answer else 0
        except ValueError:
            failed.append(f"{rule.db}: `{rule.query}` answered {answer!r}, which is not a count")
            continue
        if count < rule.min:
            failed.append(
                f"{rule.db}: `{rule.query}` is {count}, expected at least {rule.min}"
            )
            continue
        logger.info(f"verified {rule.db}: {rule.query} = {count} (>= {rule.min})")
    return tuple(failed)


def write_marker(
    plan: SqlPlan, *, container: str, client: str, password: str, exec_stdin: ExecStdin
) -> None:
    """Record that this plan finished, in `<marker_db>.yulon_install`.

    Only ever called after `verify()` returned `()`; the row is what
    `MarkerGate.probe()` reads as `imported`. The plan hash is stored so an
    upgrade that changes the plan can be SEEN in the log — it is never a
    reason to re-import (see the probe's table: a mismatched hash is a
    finished import from an older plan).
    """
    text = (
        f"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `{plan.marker_db}`.`{MARKER_TABLE}` "
        "(plan_hash CHAR(16) NOT NULL, finished_unix BIGINT NOT NULL);\n"
        f"INSERT INTO `{plan.marker_db}`.`{MARKER_TABLE}` (plan_hash, finished_unix) "
        f"VALUES ('{plan.plan_hash()}', {int(time.time())});\n"
    )
    _run_sql(
        text,
        what="writing the import marker",
        container=container,
        client=client,
        password=password,
        schema=plan.marker_db,
        exec_stdin=exec_stdin,
    )


def _run_sql(
    text: str,
    *,
    what: str,
    container: str,
    client: str,
    password: str,
    schema: str | None,
    exec_stdin: ExecStdin,
) -> None:
    """One SQL script over stdin; a non-zero exit is an `InstallerError` with the last stderr line."""
    proc = exec_stdin(
        container,
        _client_argv(client, schema),
        io.BytesIO(text.encode("utf-8")),
        env={"MYSQL_PWD": password},
    )
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        reason = _last_line((proc.stderr or "").splitlines()) or f"exit {proc.returncode}"
        raise InstallerError(f"The import stopped while {what}: {reason}")


def _schema_named(name: str, schemas: Mapping[str, str]) -> str:
    try:
        return schemas[name]
    except KeyError:
        raise InstallerError(
            f"the SQL plan names {name!r}, which is not one of this game's databases "
            f"({', '.join(schemas)}). That is a catalog error."
        ) from None


def _refuse_quotes(value: str, what: str) -> None:
    """A value spliced into `'...'` must not carry a quote or a backslash; there is no escaping here.

    Generated passwords are hex and the fixed one passed `composegen._refuse_unsafe`,
    so this is a second lock on a door that should already be shut.
    """
    if "'" in value or "\\" in value:
        raise InstallerError(f"{what} contains a quote or backslash, which cannot be used here.")
```

### Task J.6: MarkerGate — the five-branch probe and the partial-only reset

**Files:**
- Modify: `yulon/catalog/families/sqlplan.py` (add `MarkerGate`, `_MARKER_LOOKUP`, `_TABLE_EXISTS`)
- Test: `tests/test_sqlplan.py` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `docker.ImportState(state, detail, complete)` (grep for `class ImportState` in `yulon/docker.py`; the field is `.state`, never `.status`), `docker.DockerCommandError`; `PlayerData(db, table, exclude_usernames)` (contract); `SqlQuery`, `ExecStdin`, `MARKER_TABLE`, `_run_sql`, `_schema_named`, `_refuse_quotes` (J.3–J.5); `native.ImportGate` Protocol (contract 7.1: `probe() -> ImportState`, `reset() -> tuple[str, ...]`) — satisfied structurally, not imported
- Produces: `MarkerGate(plan, *, container, client, password, schemas, sql_query, exec_stdin)` with `.probe()` and `.reset()` (contract) — `cmangos.py` passes it to `stage_import(ctx, gate, None)`, which per A7 runs the five-branch table (skip on `imported`, refuse `unreadable`/`populated`, `gate.reset()` on `partial`) and then returns so the family applies the SQL itself. `schemas` is the same identity mapping J.3 describes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — append to `tests/test_sqlplan.py`:

```python
from yulon.catalog.catalog import PlayerData

GATE_PLAN = SqlPlan(
    create=("mangos", "realmd", "characters", "logs"),
    phases=PLAN.phases,
    verify=PLAN.verify,
    player_data=(
        PlayerData(db="characters", table="characters"),
        PlayerData(db="realmd", table="account", exclude_usernames=("ADMINISTRATOR", "GAMEMASTER", "MODERATOR", "PLAYER")),
    ),
    marker_db="mangos",
)


class _Server:
    """`docker.sql_query` + `docker.exec_stdin` over one fake server's state.

    Answers are shaped like `--batch --skip-column-names` output. `rows` is
    the full count of a table, `seeded` how many of those carry an excluded
    username, so a `WHERE username NOT IN (...)` query answers the difference.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        databases: Sequence[str] = (),
        tables: Mapping[str, Sequence[str]] | None = None,
        marker_hash: str | None = None,
        rows: Mapping[tuple[str, str], int] | None = None,
        seeded: Mapping[tuple[str, str], int] | None = None,
        down: str = "",
    ) -> None:
        self.databases = list(databases)
        self.tables = {name: set(names) for name, names in (tables or {}).items()}
        if marker_hash is not None:
            self.tables.setdefault("mangos", set()).add("yulon_install")
        self.marker_hash = marker_hash
        self.rows = dict(rows or {})
        self.seeded = dict(seeded or {})
        self.down = down
        self.asked: list[tuple[str | None, str]] = []
        self.dropped: list[str] = []

    def query(self, container: str, client: str, password: str, schema: str | None, statement: str) -> str:
        self.asked.append((schema, statement))
        if self.down:
            raise docker.DockerCommandError(self.down)
        if statement == "SHOW DATABASES":
            return "\n".join(["information_schema", "mysql", *self.databases]) + "\n"
        exists = re.search(r"table_schema='([^']+)' AND table_name='([^']+)'", statement)
        if exists:
            return "1\n" if exists.group(2) in self.tables.get(exists.group(1), set()) else "0\n"
        if statement.startswith("SELECT plan_hash"):
            return f"{self.marker_hash}\n" if self.marker_hash else ""
        count = re.search(r"SELECT COUNT\(\*\) FROM `([^`]+)`\.`([^`]+)`", statement)
        if count:
            key = (count.group(1), count.group(2))
            total = self.rows.get(key, 0)
            return f"{total - self.seeded.get(key, 0) if 'NOT IN' in statement else total}\n"
        raise AssertionError(f"unexpected query: {statement}")

    def exec_stdin(self, container: str, argv: Sequence[str], source: BinaryIO, *, env: Mapping[str, str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
        for name in re.findall(r"DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS `([^`]+)`", source.read().decode()):
            self.dropped.append(name)
            self.databases = [d for d in self.databases if d != name]
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(list(argv), 0, "", "")


def _gate(server: _Server) -> sqlplan.MarkerGate:
    return sqlplan.MarkerGate(GATE_PLAN, container="tbc-db", client="mariadb", password="pw", schemas=SCHEMAS, sql_query=server.query, exec_stdin=server.exec_stdin)


ALL = ("mangos", "realmd", "characters", "logs")


def test_probe_absent_when_no_plan_schema_exists() -> None:
    state = _gate(_Server(databases=["playerbots_other"])).probe()
    assert state.state == "absent"
    assert "mangos" in state.detail


def test_probe_imported_when_the_marker_row_exists() -> None:
    server = _Server(databases=ALL, marker_hash=GATE_PLAN.plan_hash(), rows={("characters", "characters"): 40})
    state = _gate(server).probe()
    assert state.state == "imported" and state.complete is True
    assert not any("COUNT(*)" in q and "characters" in q for _, q in server.asked), "a marker settles it before any player-data count"


def test_probe_hash_mismatch_is_still_imported_never_partial(caplog: pytest.LogCaptureFixture) -> None:
    server = _Server(databases=ALL, marker_hash="0123456789abcdef")
    state = _gate(server).probe()
    assert state.state == "imported"
    assert "older" in state.detail and "0123456789abcdef" in state.detail
    assert server.dropped == []


def test_probe_populated_on_characters() -> None:
    server = _Server(databases=ALL, tables={"characters": ["characters"], "realmd": ["account"]}, rows={("characters", "characters"): 3})
    state = _gate(server).probe()
    assert state.state == "populated"
    assert "3 rows in characters.characters" in state.detail


def test_probe_counts_accounts_without_the_seeded_usernames() -> None:
    tables = {"characters": ["characters"], "realmd": ["account"]}
    seeded_only = _Server(databases=ALL, tables=tables, rows={("realmd", "account"): 4}, seeded={("realmd", "account"): 4})
    assert _gate(seeded_only).probe().state == "partial"
    where = [q for _, q in seeded_only.asked if "`realmd`.`account`" in q]
    assert where and "WHERE username NOT IN ('ADMINISTRATOR', 'GAMEMASTER', 'MODERATOR', 'PLAYER')" in where[0]
    one_more = _Server(databases=ALL, tables=tables, rows={("realmd", "account"): 5}, seeded={("realmd", "account"): 4})
    state = _gate(one_more).probe()
    assert state.state == "populated" and "1 rows in realmd.account" in state.detail


def test_probe_partial_when_schemas_exist_without_marker_or_players() -> None:
    server = _Server(databases=["mangos", "realmd"], tables={"realmd": ["account"]})
    state = _gate(server).probe()
    assert state.state == "partial"
    assert "mangos, realmd" in state.detail and "no import marker" in state.detail


def test_probe_unreadable_names_the_volume_and_the_remove_action() -> None:
    state = _gate(_Server(down="ERROR 1045 (28000): Access denied for user 'root'@'localhost'")).probe()
    assert state.state == "unreadable"
    assert "Access denied" in state.detail and "different password" in state.detail and "Remove" in state.detail


def test_reset_drops_exactly_the_present_plan_schemas_from_partial() -> None:
    server = _Server(databases=["mangos", "realmd", "somebody_else"], tables={"realmd": ["account"]})
    assert _gate(server).reset() == ("mangos", "realmd")
    assert server.dropped == ["mangos", "realmd"]
    assert server.databases == ["somebody_else"]


def test_reset_refuses_populated_and_drops_nothing() -> None:
    populated = _Server(databases=ALL, tables={"characters": ["characters"]}, rows={("characters", "characters"): 1})
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="player data"):
        _gate(populated).reset()
    assert populated.dropped == []
    imported = _Server(databases=ALL, marker_hash=GATE_PLAN.plan_hash())
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="already imported"):
        _gate(imported).reset()
    with pytest.raises(InstallerError, match="could not be asked"):
        _gate(_Server(down="no route")).reset()


def test_reset_from_absent_drops_nothing() -> None:
    server = _Server()
    assert _gate(server).reset() == ()
    assert server.dropped == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write minimal implementation** — append to `yulon/catalog/families/sqlplan.py`:

```python
_TABLE_EXISTS = (
    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.tables "
    "WHERE table_schema='{schema}' AND table_name='{table}'"
)
_MARKER_LOOKUP = "SELECT plan_hash FROM `{schema}`.`{table}` ORDER BY finished_unix DESC LIMIT 1"


class MarkerGate:
    """`native.ImportGate` for a plan with a marker: the probe's five branches, and a reset.

    The same table the AzerothCore probe answers (`controller_wow_wotlk/repair.py`),
    with the evidence this family actually has: a marker row `write_marker()`
    left, and the plan's `player_data` tables. Ordered by what a wrong answer
    would cost — `imported` is checked before `populated` because a marker is
    proof, and `populated` before `partial` because the only destructive step
    on this path (`reset()`) must never see a database with a person's rows.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        plan: SqlPlan,
        *,
        container: str,
        client: str,
        password: str,
        schemas: Mapping[str, str],
        sql_query: SqlQuery,
        exec_stdin: ExecStdin,
    ) -> None:
        self._plan = plan
        self._container = container
        self._client = client
        self._password = password
        self._schemas = schemas
        self._sql_query = sql_query
        self._exec_stdin = exec_stdin

    def probe(self) -> docker.ImportState:
        """What state the plan's schemas are in. Never raises.

        | none of the plan's schemas exist            | absent     |
        | a marker row exists (any hash)              | imported   |
        | rows in a `player_data` table beyond seeds  | populated  |
        | schemas exist, no marker, no player rows    | partial    |
        | the client cannot answer                    | unreadable |

        A marker whose hash differs from this plan's is a finished import by an
        older plan: logged, reported as imported, never re-run — an app upgrade
        must not `DROP realmd` on a server with accounts.
        """
        names = self._names()
        try:
            present = [name for name in names if name in self._databases()]
            if not present:
                return docker.ImportState(
                    "absent", f"none of {', '.join(names)} exists on this server yet"
                )
            marker = self._marker(present)
            if marker is not None:
                detail = f"{self._schemas[self._plan.marker_db]}.{MARKER_TABLE} records a finished import"
                if marker != self._plan.plan_hash():
                    detail += (
                        f" by an older plan ({marker}, this app's is {self._plan.plan_hash()}); "
                        "it is kept as it is"
                    )
                    logger.info(detail)
                return docker.ImportState("imported", detail, complete=True)
            populated = self._player_rows(present)
        except docker.DockerCommandError as exc:
            return docker.ImportState(
                "unreadable",
                f"the database could not be asked what state it is in ({exc}). If its volume "
                "was created by an earlier install with a different password, use Remove to "
                "delete this install's containers and volumes, then install again.",
            )
        if populated:
            return docker.ImportState("populated", populated)
        return docker.ImportState(
            "partial",
            f"{', '.join(present)} exist{'s' if len(present) == 1 else ''} but there is no "
            "import marker, so the import never finished",
        )

    def reset(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """Drop the plan's schemas that exist — only from `partial`, only the plan's own.

        Returns the schemas dropped (`()` from `absent`). Refuses every other
        state by name: `populated` is somebody's server, `imported` needs no
        reset, `unreadable` proves nothing. Asked again here rather than
        trusting the caller, because this is the one function on the path that
        destroys anything.
        """
        state = self.probe()
        if state.state == "absent":
            return ()
        if state.state == "populated":
            raise InstallerError(
                f"This install holds player data ({state.detail}), so nothing was dropped."
            )
        if state.state == "imported":
            raise InstallerError(f"This install is already imported ({state.detail}); nothing to reset.")
        if state.state == "unreadable":
            raise InstallerError(
                f"The databases could not be asked what state they are in, so nothing was "
                f"dropped. {state.detail}"
            )
        doomed = [name for name in self._names() if name in self._databases()]
        for name in doomed:
            logger.warning(f"dropping {name}: it was left half-written by an interrupted import")
        _run_sql(
            "".join(f"DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS `{name}`;\n" for name in doomed),
            what="clearing the half-written databases",
            container=self._container,
            client=self._client,
            password=self._password,
            schema=None,
            exec_stdin=self._exec_stdin,
        )
        left = [name for name in doomed if name in self._databases()]
        if left:
            raise InstallerError(
                f"{', '.join(left)} could not be dropped, so the import was not re-run."
            )
        return tuple(doomed)

    def _names(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """The plan's schemas on the server, in `create` order then any other it mentions."""
        seen: dict[str, None] = {}
        mentioned = [
            *self._plan.create,
            self._plan.marker_db,
            *(rule.db for rule in self._plan.verify),
            *(data.db for data in self._plan.player_data),
        ]
        for phase in self._plan.phases:
            mentioned += [phase.into] if phase.into else list(phase.into_each or {})
        for name in mentioned:
            seen.setdefault(_schema_named(name, self._schemas), None)
        return tuple(seen)

    def _query(self, schema: str | None, statement: str) -> str:
        return self._sql_query(self._container, self._client, self._password, schema, statement)

    def _databases(self) -> set[str]:
        return {line.strip() for line in self._query(None, "SHOW DATABASES").splitlines()}

    def _table_exists(self, schema: str, table: str) -> bool:
        answer = self._query(schema, _TABLE_EXISTS.format(schema=schema, table=table)).strip()
        return answer.splitlines()[:1] not in ([], ["0"])

    def _marker(self, present: Sequence[str]) -> str | None:
        """The newest marker row's hash, or None when the table (or its schema) is not there."""
        marker_db = self._schemas[self._plan.marker_db]
        if marker_db not in present or not self._table_exists(marker_db, MARKER_TABLE):
            return None
        answer = self._query(
            marker_db, _MARKER_LOOKUP.format(schema=marker_db, table=MARKER_TABLE)
        ).strip()
        return answer.splitlines()[0].strip() if answer else None

    def _player_rows(self, present: Sequence[str]) -> str:
        """`"3 rows in characters.characters"` for what a person (not a seed) made; `""` for none."""
        said: list[str] = []
        for data in self._plan.player_data:
            schema = self._schemas[data.db]
            if schema not in present or not self._table_exists(schema, data.table):
                continue
            statement = f"SELECT COUNT(*) FROM `{schema}`.`{data.table}`"
            if data.exclude_usernames:
                for name in data.exclude_usernames:
                    _refuse_quotes(name, "an excluded username")
                names = ", ".join(f"'{name}'" for name in data.exclude_usernames)
                statement += f" WHERE username NOT IN ({names})"
            answer = self._query(schema, statement).strip()
            try:
                rows = int(answer.splitlines()[0]) if answer else 0
            except ValueError:
                raise docker.DockerCommandError(
                    f"{schema}.{data.table} answered {answer!r}, which is not a count"
                ) from None
            if rows:
                said.append(f"{rows} rows in {schema}.{data.table}")
        return ", ".join(said)
```
